+def _get_log_cap() -> int:

+        config = load_config()

+    """Append a structured entry to the plan's execution log."""
+    log = plan.get("execution_log", [])

+        "finding_ids": finding_ids or [],
+        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
+        "actor": actor,
+        "note": note,
+        "detail": detail or {},
+    }
+    log.append(entry)
+    cap = _get_log_cap()

+def merge_clusters(
+    plan: PlanModel, source_name: str, target_name: str
+) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
+    """Move all source findings to target, copy missing metadata, delete source.
+
+    Returns ``(added_count, source_finding_ids)``.
+    """
+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
+    if source_name == target_name:
+        raise ValueError("Cannot merge a cluster into itself")
+    source = plan["clusters"].get(source_name)
+    if source is None:
+        raise ValueError(f"Source cluster {source_name!r} does not exist")
+    target = plan["clusters"].get(target_name)
+    if target is None:
+        raise ValueError(f"Target cluster {target_name!r} does not exist")
+
+    source_ids = list(source.get("finding_ids", []))
+    target_ids: list[str] = target["finding_ids"]
+    now = utc_now()
+
+    # Add source findings to target (deduplicate)
+    existing = set(target_ids)
+    added = 0
+    for fid in source_ids:
+        if fid not in existing:
+            target_ids.append(fid)
+            existing.add(fid)
+            added += 1
+        # Update override to point to target cluster
+        overrides = plan["overrides"]
+        if fid not in overrides:
+            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
+        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = target_name
+        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
+
+    # Copy metadata from source if target is missing them
+    if not target.get("description") and source.get("description"):
+        target["description"] = source["description"]
+    if not target.get("action_steps") and source.get("action_steps"):
+        target["action_steps"] = list(source["action_steps"])
+    if not target.get("action") and source.get("action"):
+        target["action"] = source["action"]
+
+    target["updated_at"] = now
+
+    # Delete source cluster
+    plan["clusters"].pop(source_name, None)
+    if plan.get("active_cluster") == source_name:
+        plan["active_cluster"] = None
+
+    return added, source_ids
+

+@dataclass
+class ReviewImportSyncResult:
+    """Summary of plan changes after a review import."""
+
+    new_ids: set[str]
+    added_to_queue: list[str]
+    triage_injected: bool
+
+
+def sync_plan_after_review_import(
+    plan: PlanModel,
+    state: StateModel,
+) -> ReviewImportSyncResult | None:
+    """Sync plan queue after review import. Pure engine function — no I/O.
+
+    Appends new finding IDs to queue_order and injects triage stages
+    if needed. Returns None when there are no new findings to sync.
+    """
+    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
+        compute_new_finding_ids,
+        sync_triage_needed,
+    )
+
+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
+    new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(plan, state)
+    if not new_ids:
+        return None
+
+    # Add new finding IDs to end of queue_order so they have position
+    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
+    existing = set(order)
+    added: list[str] = []
+    for fid in sorted(new_ids):
+        if fid not in existing:
+            order.append(fid)
+            added.append(fid)
+
+    # Inject triage stages if needed
+    triage_result = sync_triage_needed(plan, state)
+    triage_injected = bool(
+        triage_result and getattr(triage_result, "injected", False)
+    )
+
+    return ReviewImportSyncResult(
+        new_ids=new_ids,
+        added_to_queue=added,
+        triage_injected=triage_injected,
+    )
+
+
 __all__ = [
     "ReconcileResult",
+    "ReviewImportSyncResult",
     "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
+    "sync_plan_after_review_import",
 ]

+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Repair
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan: PlanModel, now: str) -> int:
+    """Clear override cluster refs that point to non-existent clusters."""
+    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
+    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
+    repaired = 0
+    for override in overrides.values():
+        cluster_name = override.get("cluster")
+        if cluster_name and cluster_name not in clusters:
+            override["cluster"] = None
+            override["updated_at"] = now
+            repaired += 1
+    return repaired
+
+

+def _current_under_target_ids(

+    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.

+    if not promoted:
+        return 0
+    last_idx = -1
+    for i, fid in enumerate(order):

+            last_idx = i
+    return last_idx + 1 if last_idx >= 0 else 0

+def compute_new_finding_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
+    """Return the set of open review/concerns finding IDs added since last triage.

+    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
+    triaged = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", meta.get("synthesized_ids", [])))
+    current = {
+        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
+        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
+    }
+    return current - triaged if triaged else set()
+

+def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
+    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
+

+    review-finding snapshot hash has drifted from the last completed triage.

+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)

+        return True
+    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
+    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
+    if not last_hash:
+        return False
+    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
+    return bool(current_hash and current_hash != last_hash)

+def enrich_with_impact(items: list[dict], dimension_scores: dict) -> None:
+    """Stamp ``estimated_impact`` on each item based on dimension-level headroom.
+
+    Impact = ``overall_per_point * headroom`` where headroom = ``100 - score``.
+    Items in dimensions with more score headroom sort first.
+    """
+    if not dimension_scores:
+        for item in items:
+            item["estimated_impact"] = 0.0
+        return
+
+    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_breakdown
+    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.impact import get_dimension_for_detector
+
+    breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores)
+    entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
+
+    # Build lookup: normalized dimension name -> {per_point, headroom}
+    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
+    for entry in entries:
+        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
+        if not name:
+            continue
+        per_point = float(entry.get("overall_per_point", 0.0))
+        score = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
+        headroom = 100.0 - score
+        dim_impact[name.lower()] = {"per_point": per_point, "headroom": headroom}
+
+    for item in items:
+        impact = _compute_item_impact(item, dim_impact, get_dimension_for_detector)
+        item["estimated_impact"] = impact
+
+
+def _compute_item_impact(
+    item: dict,
+    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]],
+    get_dimension_for_detector,
+) -> float:
+    """Compute impact value for a single queue item."""
+    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
+
+    # Subjective items (synthetic dimensions or subjective findings):
+    # look up by detail.dimension_name
+    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
+        dim_name = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
+        entry = dim_impact.get(dim_name.lower())
+        if entry:
+            return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
+        return 0.0
+
+    # Mechanical findings: use detector -> dimension mapping
+    detector = item.get("detector", "")
+    if detector:
+        dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
+        if dimension:
+            entry = dim_impact.get(dimension.name.lower())
+            if entry:
+                return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
+
+    return 0.0
+

--- a/desloppify/engine/_state/filtering.py

@@ -3,6 +3,7 @@
 from __future__ import annotations
 
 import fnmatch
+import re
 
 __all__ = [
     "finding_in_scan_scope",
@@ -100,8 +101,13 @@ def matched_ignore_pattern(
                 return pattern
             continue
 
-        if file == pattern or file == rel(pattern):
-            return pattern
+        raw_base = pattern.rstrip("/")
+        rel_base = rel(pattern).rstrip("/")
+        for base in (raw_base, rel_base):
+            if not base:
+                continue
+            if file == base or file.startswith(base + "/"):
+                return pattern
 
     return None
 
@@ -169,15 +175,34 @@ def make_finding(
     }
 
 
+_HEX8_RE = re.compile(r'^[0-9a-f]{8}$')
+
+
 def _matches_pattern(finding_id: str, finding: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
-    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, or path."""
-    return (
-        finding_id == pattern
-        or ("*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern))
-        or ("::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern))
-        or (
-            finding.get("detector") == pattern
-            or finding["file"] == pattern
-            or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
-        )
-    )
+    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, suffix, or path."""
+    if finding_id == pattern:
+        return True
+    if "*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern):
+        return True
+    if "::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern):
+        return True
+    if _HEX8_RE.match(pattern) and finding_id.endswith("::" + pattern):
+        return True
+    if (
+        finding.get("detector") == pattern
+        or finding["file"] == pattern
+        or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
+    ):
+        return True
+
+    # Name-segment fallback: bare name matches the last ::segment of the ID
+    if "::" not in pattern and "::" in finding_id:
+        segments = finding_id.split("::")
+        name_segment = segments[-1]
+        if name_segment == pattern:
+            return True
+        # For hashed IDs, also match the descriptive name (second-to-last segment)
+        if len(segments) >= 3 and _HEX8_RE.match(name_segment) and segments[-2] == pattern:
+            return True
+
+    return False

diff --git a/desloppify/core/query.py b/desloppify/core/query.py
index 1d5e4b0..d5029f5 100644
--- a/desloppify/core/query.py
+++ b/desloppify/core/query.py
@@ -14,27 +14,210 @@ from desloppify.state import json_default
 
 logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
 
+QUERY_PAYLOAD_MAX_BYTES = 2_000_000
+QUERY_ITEMS_SOFT_LIMIT = 200
+QUERY_CLUSTER_MEMBER_LIMIT = 10
+QUERY_TEXT_LIMIT = 400
+
+
+def _payload_size_bytes(payload: dict) -> int:
+    """Return UTF-8 byte size for a JSON-serializable payload."""
+    return len(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, default=json_default).encode("utf-8"))
+
+
+def _truncate_text(value: object, *, limit: int | None = None) -> object:
+    """Return text truncated to a fixed character budget."""
+    if not isinstance(value, str):
+        return value
+    limit_value = QUERY_TEXT_LIMIT if limit is None else limit
+    if len(value) <= limit_value:
+        return value
+    return value[: limit_value - 1] + "…"
+
+
+def _lightweight_item(item: object) -> object:
+    """Return a lighter-weight queue item safe for query payload persistence."""
+    if not isinstance(item, dict):
+        return item
+
+    light = dict(item)
+    light["summary"] = _truncate_text(light.get("summary"))
+
+    detail = light.get("detail")
+    if isinstance(detail, dict):
+        compact_detail: dict = {}
+        for key, value in detail.items():
+            if isinstance(value, str):
+                compact_detail[key] = _truncate_text(value, limit=200)
+            elif isinstance(value, list):
+                compact_detail[key] = value[:20]
+            elif isinstance(value, dict):
+                compact_detail[key] = {"truncated": True, "keys": len(value)}
+            else:
+                compact_detail[key] = value
+        light["detail"] = compact_detail
+
+    if light.get("kind") == "cluster":
+        members = light.get("members")
+        if isinstance(members, list) and len(members) > QUERY_CLUSTER_MEMBER_LIMIT:
+            light["members"] = members[:QUERY_CLUSTER_MEMBER_LIMIT]
+            light["members_truncated"] = True
+            light["members_sample_limit"] = QUERY_CLUSTER_MEMBER_LIMIT
+
+    return light
+
+
+def _minimal_payload(payload: dict, *, max_bytes: int) -> dict:
+    """Build a minimal fallback payload when budget cannot be met."""
+    minimal: dict = {
+        "command": payload.get("command"),
+        "queue": payload.get("queue", {}),
+        "overall_score": payload.get("overall_score"),
+        "objective_score": payload.get("objective_score"),
+        "strict_score": payload.get("strict_score"),
+    }
+
+    sample_items: list[dict] = []
+    items = payload.get("items")
+    if isinstance(items, list):
+        for item in items[:20]:
+            if not isinstance(item, dict):
+                continue
+            sample_items.append(
+                {
+                    "id": item.get("id"),
+                    "kind": item.get("kind"),
+                    "detector": item.get("detector"),
+                    "file": item.get("file"),
+                    "summary": _truncate_text(item.get("summary"), limit=200),
+                }
+            )
+    minimal["items"] = sample_items
+
+    if "config" in payload:
+        minimal["config"] = payload["config"]
+    if "config_error" in payload:
+        minimal["config_error"] = payload["config_error"]
+
+    minimal["query_truncated"] = {
+        "mode": "minimal",
+        "max_bytes": max_bytes,
+        "items_sample": len(sample_items),
+    }
+    return _fit_payload_to_budget(minimal, max_bytes=max_bytes)
+
+
+def _fit_payload_to_budget(payload: dict, *, max_bytes: int) -> dict:
+    """Shrink payload further until it fits max_bytes."""
+    trimmed = dict(payload)
+
+    items = trimmed.get("items")
+    if isinstance(items, list):
+        keep = len(items)
+        while keep > 0 and _payload_size_bytes(trimmed) > max_bytes:
+            keep = max(0, keep // 2)
+            trimmed["items"] = items[:keep]
+
+    if _payload_size_bytes(trimmed) > max_bytes and "config" in trimmed:
+        trimmed.pop("config", None)
+        trimmed["config_truncated"] = True
+
+    if _payload_size_bytes(trimmed) > max_bytes and "queue" in trimmed:
+        trimmed["queue"] = {}
+
+    if _payload_size_bytes(trimmed) > max_bytes:
+        return {
+            "command": payload.get("command"),
+            "query_truncated": {
+                "mode": "minimal",
+                "max_bytes": max_bytes,
+                "overflow": True,
+            },
+        }
+
+    query_meta = trimmed.get("query_truncated")
+    if isinstance(query_meta, dict) and isinstance(trimmed.get("items"), list):
+        query_meta["items_sample"] = len(trimmed["items"])
+    return trimmed
+
+
+def _enforce_payload_budget(
+    payload: dict,
+    *,
+    max_bytes: int | None = None,
+) -> tuple[dict, list[str]]:
+    """Bound payload size with deterministic truncation steps."""
+    budget = QUERY_PAYLOAD_MAX_BYTES if max_bytes is None else max_bytes
+    if _payload_size_bytes(payload) <= budget:
+        return payload, []
+
+    notes: list[str] = []
+    trimmed = dict(payload)
+
+    items = trimmed.get("items")
+    if isinstance(items, list):
+        original_count = len(items)
+        limited_items = items[:QUERY_ITEMS_SOFT_LIMIT]
+        if len(limited_items) < original_count:
+            notes.append(f"items:{original_count}->{len(limited_items)}")
+        trimmed["items"] = [_lightweight_item(item) for item in limited_items]
+
+    if _payload_size_bytes(trimmed) > budget and isinstance(trimmed.get("narrative"), dict):
+        trimmed["narrative"] = {"truncated": True}
+        notes.append("narrative")
+
+    plan = trimmed.get("plan")
+    if _payload_size_bytes(trimmed) > budget and isinstance(plan, dict):
+        clusters = plan.get("clusters")
+        if isinstance(clusters, list) and len(clusters) > 100:
+            compact_plan = dict(plan)
+            compact_plan["clusters"] = clusters[:100]
+            compact_plan["clusters_truncated"] = True
+            trimmed["plan"] = compact_plan
+            notes.append("plan.clusters")
+
+    if _payload_size_bytes(trimmed) > budget:
+        trimmed = _minimal_payload(trimmed, max_bytes=budget)
+        notes.append("minimal")
+
+    if notes:
+        meta = trimmed.get("query_truncated")
+        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
+            meta = {}
+        meta["max_bytes"] = budget
+        meta["actual_bytes"] = _payload_size_bytes(trimmed)
+        meta["applied"] = notes
+        trimmed["query_truncated"] = meta
+
+    return trimmed, notes
+

1→"""Migration/default helpers for living plan schema payloads."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→
    10→def ensure_container_types(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    11→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
    12→        plan["queue_order"] = []
    13→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
    14→        plan["deferred"] = []
    15→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
    16→        plan["skipped"] = {}
    17→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
    18→        plan["overrides"] = {}
    19→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
    20→        plan["clusters"] = {}
    21→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
    22→        plan["superseded"] = {}
    23→    if not isinstance(plan.get("promoted_ids"), list):
    24→        plan["promoted_ids"] = []
    25→    if not isinstance(plan.get("plan_start_scores"), dict):
    26→        plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    27→    if not isinstance(plan.get("execution_log"), list):
    28→        plan["execution_log"] = []
    29→    if not isinstance(plan.get("epic_triage_meta"), dict):
    30→        plan["epic_triage_meta"] = {}
    31→
    32→
    33→def migrate_deferred_to_skipped(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    34→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
    35→    skipped: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = plan["skipped"]
    36→    if not deferred:
    37→        return
    38→
    39→    now = utc_now()
    40→    for finding_id in list(deferred):
    41→        if finding_id in skipped:
    42→            continue
    43→        skipped[finding_id] = {
    44→            "finding_id": finding_id,
    45→            "kind": "temporary",
    46→            "reason": None,
    47→            "note": None,
    48→            "attestation": None,
    49→            "created_at": now,
    50→            "review_after": None,
    51→            "skipped_at_scan": 0,
    52→        }
    53→    deferred.clear()
    54→
    55→
    56→def normalize_cluster_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    57→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
    58→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
    59→            continue
    60→        if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
    61→            cluster["finding_ids"] = []
    62→        cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
    63→        cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
    64→        cluster.setdefault("action", None)
    65→        cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
    66→
    67→
    68→def migrate_epics_to_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    69→    """Migrate v3 top-level ``epics`` dict into ``clusters`` (v4 unification)."""
    70→    epics = plan.pop("epics", None)
    71→    if not isinstance(epics, dict) or not epics:
    72→        return
    73→    clusters = plan["clusters"]
    74→    now = utc_now()
    75→    for name, epic in epics.items():
    76→        if not isinstance(epic, dict):
    77→            continue
    78→        if name in clusters:
    79→            continue
    80→        clusters[name] = {
    81→            "name": name,
    82→            "description": epic.get("thesis", ""),
    83→            "finding_ids": epic.get("finding_ids", []),
    84→            "auto": True,
    85→            "cluster_key": f"epic::{name}",
    86→            "action": f"desloppify plan focus {name}",
    87→            "user_modified": False,
    88→            "created_at": epic.get("created_at", now),
    89→            "updated_at": epic.get("updated_at", now),
    90→            "thesis": epic.get("thesis", ""),
    91→            "direction": epic.get("direction", "simplify"),
    92→            "root_cause": epic.get("root_cause", ""),
    93→            "supersedes": epic.get("supersedes", []),
    94→            "dismissed": epic.get("dismissed", []),
    95→            "agent_safe": epic.get("agent_safe", False),
    96→            "dependency_order": epic.get("dependency_order", 999),
    97→            "action_steps": epic.get("action_steps", []),
    98→            "source_clusters": epic.get("source_clusters", []),
    99→            "status": epic.get("status", "pending"),
   100→            "triage_version": epic.get("triage_version", epic.get("synthesis_version", 0)),
   101→        }
   102→
   103→
   104→def migrate_v5_to_v6(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   105→    """Migrate v5 → v6: unified queue system."""
   106→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   107→        TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
   108→        WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
   109→    )
   110→
   111→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   112→
   113→    # Handle legacy synthesis::pending or triage::pending
   114→    for legacy_pending in ("synthesis::pending", "triage::pending"):
   115→        if legacy_pending in order:
   116→            idx = order.index(legacy_pending)
   117→            order.remove(legacy_pending)
   118→            meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {}))
   119→            confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})).keys())
   120→            stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   121→            to_inject = [
   122→                stage_id
   123→                for stage_id, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names)
   124→                if name not in confirmed and stage_id not in order
   125→            ]
   126→            for offset, stage_id in enumerate(to_inject):
   127→                order.insert(idx + offset, stage_id)
   128→            break
   129→
   130→    if plan.pop("pending_plan_gate", False):
   131→        if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID not in order:
   132→            insert_at = 0
   133→            for idx, finding_id in enumerate(order):
   134→                if finding_id.startswith("triage::") or finding_id.startswith("synthesis::"):
   135→                    insert_at = idx + 1
   136→            order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   137→    else:
   138→        plan.pop("pending_plan_gate", None)
   139→
   140→
   141→def migrate_synthesis_to_triage(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   142→    """Migrate synthesis::* → triage::* naming throughout the plan.
   143→
   144→    - Renames ``synthesis::*`` IDs to ``triage::*`` in ``queue_order`` and ``skipped``
   145→    - Renames ``epic_synthesis_meta`` key to ``epic_triage_meta``
   146→    - Renames ``synthesis_stages`` to ``triage_stages`` inside that meta dict
   147→    - Renames ``synthesized_ids`` to ``triaged_ids`` inside that meta dict
   148→    - Renames ``synthesis_version`` to ``triage_version`` in cluster dicts
   149→    """
   150→    # Rename synthesis::* IDs in queue_order
   151→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   152→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   153→        if fid.startswith("synthesis::"):
   154→            order[i] = "triage::" + fid[len("synthesis::"):]
   155→
   156→    # Rename synthesis::* IDs in skipped
   157→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   158→    synth_keys = [k for k in skipped if k.startswith("synthesis::")]
   159→    for old_key in synth_keys:
   160→        new_key = "triage::" + old_key[len("synthesis::"):]
   161→        entry = skipped.pop(old_key)
   162→        if isinstance(entry, dict):
   163→            entry["finding_id"] = new_key
   164→        skipped[new_key] = entry
   165→
   166→    # Rename epic_synthesis_meta → epic_triage_meta
   167→    if "epic_synthesis_meta" in plan:
   168→        meta = plan.pop("epic_synthesis_meta")
   169→        if isinstance(meta, dict):
   170→            # Rename synthesis_stages → triage_stages
   171→            if "synthesis_stages" in meta:
   172→                meta["triage_stages"] = meta.pop("synthesis_stages")
   173→            # Rename synthesized_ids → triaged_ids
   174→            if "synthesized_ids" in meta:
   175→                meta["triaged_ids"] = meta.pop("synthesized_ids")
   176→        plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   177→
   178→    # Rename synthesized_out → triaged_out skip kind
   179→    for entry in skipped.values():
   180→        if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("kind") == "synthesized_out":
   181→            entry["kind"] = "triaged_out"
   182→
   183→    # Rename synthesis_version → triage_version in clusters
   184→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   185→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   186→            continue
   187→        if "synthesis_version" in cluster:
   188→            cluster["triage_version"] = cluster.pop("synthesis_version")
   189→
   190→
   191→__all__ = [
   192→    "ensure_container_types",
   193→    "migrate_deferred_to_skipped",
   194→    "migrate_epics_to_clusters",
   195→    "migrate_synthesis_to_triage",
   196→    "migrate_v5_to_v6",
   197→    "normalize_cluster_defaults",
   198→]
   199→
   200→

1→"""Health score aggregation helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
     6→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
     7→    MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS,
     8→    MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION,
     9→    MIN_SAMPLE,
    10→    SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS,
    11→    SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION,
    12→)
    13→
    14→
    15→def _normalize_dimension_name(name: str) -> str:
    16→    return " ".join(str(name).strip().lower().split())
    17→
    18→
    19→def _mechanical_dimension_weight(name: str) -> float:
    20→    return float(
    21→        MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS.get(
    22→            _normalize_dimension_name(name),
    23→            1.0,
    24→        )
    25→    )
    26→
    27→
    28→def _subjective_dimension_weight(name: str, data: dict) -> float:
    29→    subjective_meta = (
    30→        data.get("detectors", {}).get("subjective_assessment", {})
    31→        if isinstance(data, dict)
    32→        else {}
    33→    )
    34→    configured = (
    35→        subjective_meta.get("configured_weight")
    36→        if isinstance(subjective_meta, dict)
    37→        else None
    38→    )
    39→    if is_numeric(configured):
    40→        return max(0.0, float(configured))
    41→
    42→    return float(
    43→        SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS.get(
    44→            _normalize_dimension_name(name),
    45→            1.0,
    46→        )
    47→    )
    48→
    49→
    50→def compute_health_breakdown(
    51→    dimension_scores: dict,
    52→    *,
    53→    score_key: str = "score",
    54→) -> dict[str, object]:
    55→    """Return pool averages and weighted contribution breakdown for score transparency."""
    56→    if not dimension_scores:
    57→        return {
    58→            "overall_score": 100.0,
    59→            "mechanical_fraction": 1.0,
    60→            "subjective_fraction": 0.0,
    61→            "mechanical_avg": 100.0,
    62→            "subjective_avg": None,
    63→            "entries": [],
    64→        }
    65→
    66→    mech_sum = 0.0
    67→    mech_weight = 0.0
    68→    subj_sum = 0.0
    69→    subj_weight = 0.0
    70→    mechanical_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
    71→    subjective_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
    72→
    73→    for name, data in dimension_scores.items():
    74→        score = float(data.get(score_key, data.get("score", 0.0)))
    75→        is_subjective = "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
    76→        if is_subjective:
    77→            configured = max(0.0, _subjective_dimension_weight(name, data))
    78→            effective = configured
    79→            subj_sum += score * effective
    80→            subj_weight += effective
    81→            subjective_rows.append(
    82→                {
    83→                    "name": str(name),
    84→                    "score": score,
    85→                    "configured_weight": configured,
    86→                    "effective_weight": effective,
    87→                }
    88→            )
    89→            continue
    90→
    91→        checks = float(data.get("checks", 0) or 0)
    92→        sample_factor = min(1.0, checks / MIN_SAMPLE) if checks > 0 else 0.0
    93→        configured = max(0.0, _mechanical_dimension_weight(name))
    94→        effective = configured * sample_factor
    95→        mech_sum += score * effective
    96→        mech_weight += effective
    97→        mechanical_rows.append(
    98→            {
    99→                "name": str(name),
   100→                "score": score,
   101→                "checks": checks,
   102→                "sample_factor": sample_factor,
   103→                "configured_weight": configured,
   104→                "effective_weight": effective,
   105→            }
   106→        )
   107→
   108→    mech_avg = (mech_sum / mech_weight) if mech_weight > 0 else 100.0
   109→    subj_avg = (subj_sum / subj_weight) if subj_weight > 0 else None
   110→
   111→    if subj_avg is None:
   112→        mechanical_fraction = 1.0
   113→        subjective_fraction = 0.0
   114→        overall_score = round(mech_avg, 1)
   115→    elif mech_weight == 0:
   116→        mechanical_fraction = 0.0
   117→        subjective_fraction = 1.0
   118→        overall_score = round(subj_avg, 1)
   119→    else:
   120→        mechanical_fraction = MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   121→        subjective_fraction = SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   122→        overall_score = round(
   123→            mech_avg * mechanical_fraction + subj_avg * subjective_fraction,
   124→            1,
   125→        )
   126→
   127→    entries: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   128→    for row in mechanical_rows:
   129→        pool_share = (
   130→            float(row["effective_weight"]) / mech_weight if mech_weight > 0 else 0.0
   131→        )
   132→        per_point = mechanical_fraction * pool_share
   133→        score = float(row["score"])
   134→        entries.append(
   135→            {
   136→                "name": str(row["name"]),
   137→                "pool": "mechanical",
   138→                "score": score,
   139→                "checks": float(row["checks"]),
   140→                "sample_factor": float(row["sample_factor"]),
   141→                "configured_weight": float(row["configured_weight"]),
   142→                "effective_weight": float(row["effective_weight"]),
   143→                "pool_share": pool_share,
   144→                "overall_per_point": per_point,
   145→                "overall_contribution": per_point * score,
   146→                "overall_drag": per_point * (100.0 - score),
   147→            }
   148→        )
   149→
   150→    for row in subjective_rows:
   151→        pool_share = (
   152→            float(row["effective_weight"]) / subj_weight if subj_weight > 0 else 0.0
   153→        )
   154→        per_point = subjective_fraction * pool_share
   155→        score = float(row["score"])
   156→        entries.append(
   157→            {
   158→                "name": str(row["name"]),
   159→                "pool": "subjective",
   160→                "score": score,
   161→                "checks": 0.0,
   162→                "sample_factor": 1.0,
   163→                "configured_weight": float(row["configured_weight"]),
   164→                "effective_weight": float(row["effective_weight"]),
   165→                "pool_share": pool_share,
   166→                "overall_per_point": per_point,
   167→                "overall_contribution": per_point * score,
   168→                "overall_drag": per_point * (100.0 - score),
   169→            }
   170→        )
   171→
   172→    return {
   173→        "overall_score": overall_score,
   174→        "mechanical_fraction": mechanical_fraction,
   175→        "subjective_fraction": subjective_fraction,
   176→        "mechanical_avg": mech_avg,
   177→        "subjective_avg": subj_avg,
   178→        "entries": entries,
   179→    }
   180→
   181→
   182→def compute_health_score(
   183→    dimension_scores: dict,
   184→    *,
   185→    score_key: str = "score",
   186→) -> float:
   187→    """Budget-weighted blend of mechanical and subjective dimension scores."""
   188→    return float(
   189→        compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores, score_key=score_key)[
   190→            "overall_score"
   191→        ]
   192→    )
   193→
   194→
   195→__all__ = ["compute_health_breakdown", "compute_health_score"]
   196→
   197→

1→"""Detector-to-score impact helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
     6→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES,
     7→    DIMENSIONS,
     8→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME,
     9→    Dimension,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_score
    12→
    13→
    14→def compute_score_impact(
    15→    dimension_scores: dict,
    16→    potentials: dict[str, int],
    17→    detector: str,
    18→    issues_to_fix: int,
    19→) -> float:
    20→    """Estimate score improvement from fixing N issues in a detector."""
    21→    target_dim = None
    22→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    23→        if detector in dim.detectors:
    24→            target_dim = dim
    25→            break
    26→    if target_dim is None or target_dim.name not in dimension_scores:
    27→        return 0.0
    28→
    29→    potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
    30→    if potential <= 0:
    31→        return 0.0
    32→
    33→    dim_data = dimension_scores[target_dim.name]
    34→    old_score = compute_health_score(dimension_scores)
    35→
    36→    det_data = dim_data["detectors"].get(detector)
    37→    if not det_data:
    38→        return 0.0
    39→
    40→    old_weighted = det_data["weighted_failures"]
    41→    new_weighted = max(0.0, old_weighted - issues_to_fix * 1.0)
    42→
    43→    total_potential = 0
    44→    total_new_weighted_failures = 0.0
    45→    for det in target_dim.detectors:
    46→        det_values = dim_data["detectors"].get(det)
    47→        if not det_values:
    48→            continue
    49→        total_potential += det_values["potential"]
    50→        if det == detector:
    51→            total_new_weighted_failures += new_weighted
    52→        else:
    53→            total_new_weighted_failures += det_values["weighted_failures"]
    54→    if total_potential <= 0:
    55→        return 0.0
    56→
    57→    new_dim_score = (
    58→        max(
    59→            0.0,
    60→            (total_potential - total_new_weighted_failures) / total_potential,
    61→        )
    62→        * 100
    63→    )
    64→
    65→    simulated = {name: dict(data) for name, data in dimension_scores.items()}
    66→    simulated[target_dim.name] = {**dim_data, "score": round(new_dim_score, 1)}
    67→    new_score = compute_health_score(simulated)
    68→    return round(new_score - old_score, 1)
    69→
    70→
    71→def get_dimension_for_detector(detector: str) -> Dimension | None:
    72→    """Look up which dimension a detector belongs to."""
    73→    policy = DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.get(detector)
    74→    if not policy or policy.dimension is None:
    75→        return None
    76→    return DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.get(policy.dimension)
    77→
    78→
    79→__all__ = ["compute_score_impact", "get_dimension_for_detector"]
    80→
    81→

1→"""Score/value accessor helpers for state schema payloads."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→
     9→def json_default(obj: Any) -> Any:
    10→    """JSON serializer fallback for known non-JSON-native state values."""
    11→    if isinstance(obj, set):
    12→        return sorted(obj)
    13→    if isinstance(obj, Path):
    14→        return str(obj).replace("\\", "/")
    15→    if hasattr(obj, "isoformat"):
    16→        return obj.isoformat()
    17→    raise TypeError(
    18→        f"Object of type {type(obj).__name__} is not JSON serializable: {obj!r}"
    19→    )
    20→
    21→
    22→def get_overall_score(state: dict[str, Any]) -> float | None:
    23→    value = state.get("overall_score")
    24→    return float(value) if isinstance(value, int | float) else None
    25→
    26→
    27→def get_objective_score(state: dict[str, Any]) -> float | None:
    28→    value = state.get("objective_score")
    29→    return float(value) if isinstance(value, int | float) else None

    31→
    32→def get_strict_score(state: dict[str, Any]) -> float | None:

    34→    return float(value) if isinstance(value, int | float) else None
    35→
    36→

1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.state import StateModel
     6→
     7→
     8→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
     9→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    10→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    11→    if not scan_history:
    12→        return set()
    13→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    14→    if not threshold:
    15→        return set()
    16→    return {
    17→        finding_id
    18→        for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    19→        if finding.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    20→    }
    21→
    22→
    23→def _enforce_workflow_stage_order(items: list[dict]) -> None:
    24→    """Re-sort workflow_stage items in-place: unblocked before blocked."""
    25→    stage_entries = [(i, item) for i, item in enumerate(items)
    26→                     if item.get("kind") == "workflow_stage"]
    27→    if len(stage_entries) <= 1:
    28→        return
    29→    sorted_stages = sorted(
    30→        [item for _, item in stage_entries],
    31→        key=lambda it: (1 if it.get("is_blocked") else 0, int(it.get("stage_index", 0))),
    32→    )
    33→    for (idx, _), replacement in zip(stage_entries, sorted_stages):
    34→        items[idx] = replacement
    35→
    36→
    37→def apply_plan_order(
    38→    items: list[dict],
    39→    plan: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    42→    cluster: str | None = None,
    43→    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    44→) -> list[dict]:
    45→    """Reorder items according to the living plan."""
    46→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    47→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    48→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    49→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    50→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    51→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    52→
    53→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    54→    for item in items:
    55→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    56→
    57→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    58→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    59→        if override.get("description"):
    60→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    61→        if override.get("note"):
    62→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    63→        if override.get("cluster"):
    64→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    65→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    66→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    67→                "name": cluster_name,
    68→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    69→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    70→            }
    71→
    72→    ordered: list[dict] = []
    73→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
    74→    for finding_id in queue_order:
    75→        if finding_id in by_id and finding_id not in skipped_ids:
    76→            ordered.append(by_id[finding_id])
    77→            ordered_ids.add(finding_id)
    78→
    79→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
    80→    remaining_existing: list[dict] = []
    81→    remaining_new: list[dict] = []
    82→    _new = new_ids or set()
    83→    for item in items:
    84→        item_id = item["id"]
    85→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
    86→            continue
    87→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
    88→            skipped_items.append(item)
    89→        elif item_id in _new:
    90→            remaining_new.append(item)
    91→        else:
    92→            remaining_existing.append(item)
    93→
    94→    result = ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new
    95→    _enforce_workflow_stage_order(result)
    96→    if include_skipped:
    97→        result = result + skipped_items
    98→
    99→    for position, item in enumerate(result):
   100→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
   101→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   102→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   103→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   104→            if skip_entry:
   105→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   106→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   107→                if skip_reason:
   108→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   109→
   110→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   111→    if effective_cluster:
   112→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   113→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   114→        if cluster_member_ids:
   115→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   116→    return result
   117→
   118→
   119→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   120→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   121→    try:
   122→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   123→
   124→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   125→        if meta:
   126→            return meta.action_type
   127→    except ImportError as exc:
   128→        _ = exc
   129→    return "manual_fix"
   130→
   131→
   132→def _build_cluster_meta(
   133→    cluster_name: str, members: list[dict], cluster_data: dict
   134→) -> dict:
   135→    """Build a cluster meta-item from its member items."""
   136→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   137→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   138→    if "desloppify fix" in action:
   139→        action_type = "auto_fix"
   140→    elif "desloppify move" in action:
   141→        action_type = "reorganize"
   142→    else:
   143→        action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   144→        if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   145→            action_type = "refactor"
   146→
   147→    stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   148→    total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   149→    if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   150→        summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   151→    else:
   152→        summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   153→
   154→    primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   155→    if not primary_command:
   156→        primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   157→
   158→    estimated_impact = max(
   159→        (m.get("estimated_impact", 0.0) for m in members), default=0.0
   160→    )
   161→
   162→    return {
   163→        "id": cluster_name,
   164→        "kind": "cluster",
   165→        "action_type": action_type,
   166→        "summary": summary,
   167→        "members": members,
   168→        "member_count": len(members),
   169→        "primary_command": primary_command,
   170→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   171→        "cluster_auto": True,
   172→        "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   173→        "confidence": "high",
   174→        "detector": detector,
   175→        "file": "",
   176→        "estimated_impact": estimated_impact,
   177→    }
   178→
   179→
   180→def collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   181→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   182→
   183→    Walks the list in order: the first member of each collapsed cluster is
   184→    replaced with its meta-item, subsequent members are skipped.  This
   185→    preserves the ordering established by sort + plan-order.
   186→    """
   187→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   188→    if not clusters:
   189→        return items
   190→
   191→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   192→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   193→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   194→            continue
   195→        for finding_id in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   196→            fid_to_cluster[finding_id] = name
   197→
   198→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   199→        return items
   200→
   201→    # Collect members per cluster (preserving encounter order)
   202→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   203→    for item in items:
   204→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   205→        if cname:
   206→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   207→
   208→    # Build meta-items only for clusters with 2+ members in the queue
   209→    meta_items: dict[str, dict] = {}
   210→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   211→        if len(members) < 2:
   212→            continue
   213→        meta_items[cname] = _build_cluster_meta(
   214→            cname, members, clusters.get(cname, {})
   215→        )
   216→
   217→    # Walk in order: replace first member of each collapsed cluster
   218→    # with its meta-item, skip subsequent members
   219→    seen_clusters: set[str] = set()
   220→    result: list[dict] = []
   221→    for item in items:
   222→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   223→        if cname and cname in meta_items:
   224→            if cname not in seen_clusters:
   225→                seen_clusters.add(cname)
   226→                result.append(meta_items[cname])
   227→            # skip individual member
   228→        else:
   229→            result.append(item)
   230→    return result
   231→
   232→
   233→__all__ = [
   234→    "apply_plan_order",
   235→    "collapse_clusters",
   236→    "new_item_ids",
   237→]
   238→
```

1→"""Registry for optional language hook modules consumed by detectors."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import importlib
     6→import logging
     7→import sys
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.paths_api import get_project_root
    10→from desloppify.languages._framework import registry_state
    11→from desloppify.languages._framework.scoped_store import ScopedDictStore
    12→
    13→_HOOK_STORE: ScopedDictStore[str, dict[str, object]] = ScopedDictStore()
    14→_LOGGER = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    15→
    16→
    17→def _current_runtime_scope() -> str | None:
    18→    try:
    19→        return str(get_project_root().resolve())
    20→    except (AttributeError, OSError, RuntimeError, TypeError, ValueError):
    21→        return None
    22→
    23→
    24→def _effective_scope(scope: str | None) -> str | None:
    25→    return registry_state.effective_scope(
    26→        scope,
    27→        fallback_scope_fn=_current_runtime_scope,
    28→    )
    29→
    30→
    31→def _scope_hooks(scope: str | None, *, create: bool = False) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
    32→    return _HOOK_STORE.bucket(scope, create=create)
    33→
    34→
    35→def set_active_scope(scope: str | None) -> None:
    36→    """Set implicit hook registry scope for subsequent operations."""
    37→    registry_state.set_active_scope(scope)
    38→
    39→
    40→def get_active_scope() -> str | None:
    41→    """Return the current implicit hook registry scope, if any."""
    42→    return registry_state.get_active_scope()
    43→
    44→
    45→def register_lang_hooks(
    46→    lang_name: str,
    47→    *,
    48→    test_coverage: object | None = None,
    49→    scope: str | None = None,
    50→) -> None:
    51→    """Register optional detector hook modules for a language."""
    52→    hooks_by_lang = _scope_hooks(_effective_scope(scope), create=True)
    53→    hooks = hooks_by_lang.setdefault(lang_name, {})
    54→    if test_coverage is not None:
    55→        hooks["test_coverage"] = test_coverage
    56→
    57→
    58→def get_lang_hook(lang_name: str | None, hook_name: str, *, scope: str | None = None) -> object | None:
    59→    """Get a previously-registered language hook module."""
    60→    if not lang_name:
    61→        return None
    62→
    63→    active_scope = _effective_scope(scope)
    64→    hooks_by_lang = _scope_hooks(active_scope)
    65→    hook = hooks_by_lang.get(lang_name, {}).get(hook_name)
    66→    if hook is not None:
    67→        return hook
    68→
    69→    module_name = f"desloppify.languages.{lang_name}"
    70→    module = sys.modules.get(module_name)
    71→
    72→    # Ensure import-time register_lang_hooks() writes into the explicit scope.
    73→    with registry_state.active_scope_context(active_scope):
    74→        # Lazy-load only the requested language package.
    75→        if module is None:
    76→            try:
    77→                importlib.import_module(module_name)
    78→            except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, RuntimeError, OSError) as exc:
    79→                _LOGGER.debug(
    80→                    "Unable to import language hook package %s: %s", lang_name, exc
    81→                )
    82→                return None
    83→        elif lang_name not in hooks_by_lang:
    84→            try:
    85→                importlib.reload(module)
    86→            except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, RuntimeError, OSError) as exc:
    87→                _LOGGER.debug(
    88→                    "Unable to reload language hook package %s: %s", lang_name, exc
    89→                )
    90→                return None
    91→
    92→    return _scope_hooks(active_scope).get(lang_name, {}).get(hook_name)
    93→
    94→
    95→def clear_lang_hooks(*, scope: str | None = None) -> None:
    96→    """Clear hook registry globally or for a specific scope."""
    97→    if scope is None:
    98→        set_active_scope(None)
    99→        _HOOK_STORE.clear()
   100→        return
   101→    _HOOK_STORE.clear(scope=scope)
   102→
   103→
   104→def clear_lang_hooks_for_tests(*, scope: str | None = None) -> None:
   105→    """Compatibility test helper for clearing hook state."""
   106→    clear_lang_hooks(scope=scope)
   107→
   108→
   109→__all__ = [
   110→    "clear_lang_hooks",
   111→    "clear_lang_hooks_for_tests",
   112→    "get_active_scope",
   113→    "get_lang_hook",
   114→    "register_lang_hooks",
   115→    "set_active_scope",
   116→]
   117→

1→"""Section render helpers for markdown planning output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→
     7→
     8→def summary_lines(stats: dict) -> list[str]:
     9→    open_count = stats.get("open", 0)
    10→    total_findings = sum(
    11→        stats.get(key, 0) for key in ("open", "fixed", "wontfix", "auto_resolved")
    12→    )
    13→    addressed = total_findings - open_count
    14→    pct = round(addressed / total_findings * 100) if total_findings else 100
    15→    return [
    16→        f"- **{open_count} open** / {total_findings} total ({pct}% addressed)",
    17→        "",
    18→    ]
    19→
    20→
    21→def addressed_section(findings: dict) -> list[str]:
    22→    addressed = [finding for finding in findings.values() if finding["status"] != "open"]
    23→    if not addressed:
    24→        return []
    25→
    26→    lines: list[str] = ["---", "## Addressed", ""]
    27→    by_status: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    28→    for finding in addressed:
    29→        by_status[finding["status"]] += 1
    30→    for status, count in sorted(by_status.items()):
    31→        lines.append(f"- **{status}**: {count}")
    32→
    33→    wontfix = [
    34→        finding
    35→        for finding in addressed
    36→        if finding["status"] == "wontfix" and finding.get("note")
    37→    ]
    38→    if wontfix:
    39→        lines.extend(["", "### Wontfix (with explanations)", ""])
    40→        for finding in wontfix[:30]:
    41→            lines.append(f"- `{finding['id']}` — {finding['note']}")
    42→    lines.append("")
    43→    return lines
    44→
    45→
    46→def render_plan_item(item: dict, override: dict) -> list[str]:
    47→    """Render a single plan item as markdown lines."""
    48→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
    49→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    50→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    51→
    52→    lines = [f"- [ ] [{confidence}] {summary}"]
    53→    description = override.get("description")
    54→    if description:
    55→        lines.append(f"      → {description}")
    56→    lines.append(f"      `{item_id}`")
    57→    note = override.get("note")
    58→    if note:
    59→        lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")
    60→    return lines
    61→
    62→
    63→def plan_user_ordered_section(
    64→    items: list[dict],
    65→    plan: dict,
    66→) -> list[str]:
    67→    """Render the user-ordered queue section, grouped by cluster."""
    68→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    69→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    70→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    71→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    72→
    73→    ordered_ids = set(queue_order) - skipped_ids
    74→    if not ordered_ids:
    75→        return []
    76→
    77→    by_id = {item.get("id"): item for item in items}
    78→    lines: list[str] = [
    79→        "---",
    80→        f"## User-Ordered Queue ({len(ordered_ids)} items)",
    81→        "",
    82→    ]
    83→
    84→    emitted: set[str] = set()
    85→    for cluster_name, cluster in clusters.items():
    86→        member_ids = [
    87→            finding_id
    88→            for finding_id in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
    89→            if finding_id in ordered_ids and finding_id in by_id
    90→        ]
    91→        if not member_ids:
    92→            continue
    93→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    94→        lines.append(f"### Cluster: {cluster_name}")
    95→        if desc:
    96→            lines.append(f"> {desc}")
    97→        lines.append("")
    98→        for finding_id in member_ids:
    99→            item = by_id.get(finding_id)
   100→            if item:
   101→                lines.extend(render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(finding_id, {})))
   102→                emitted.add(finding_id)
   103→        lines.append("")
   104→
   105→    unclustered = [
   106→        finding_id
   107→        for finding_id in queue_order
   108→        if finding_id in ordered_ids and finding_id not in emitted and finding_id in by_id
   109→    ]
   110→    if unclustered:
   111→        if any(cluster.get("finding_ids") for cluster in clusters.values()):
   112→            lines.append("### (unclustered ordered items)")
   113→            lines.append("")
   114→        for finding_id in unclustered:
   115→            item = by_id.get(finding_id)
   116→            if item:
   117→                lines.extend(render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(finding_id, {})))
   118→        lines.append("")
   119→    return lines
   120→
   121→
   122→def plan_skipped_section(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   123→    """Render the skipped items section, grouped by kind."""
   124→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   125→    if not skipped:
   126→        return []
   127→
   128→    by_id = {item.get("id"): item for item in items}
   129→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   130→
   131→    by_kind: dict[str, list[str]] = {"temporary": [], "permanent": [], "false_positive": []}
   132→    for finding_id, entry in skipped.items():
   133→        kind = entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   134→        by_kind.setdefault(kind, []).append(finding_id)
   135→
   136→    kind_labels = {
   137→        "temporary": "Skipped Temporarily",
   138→        "permanent": "Wontfix (permanent)",
   139→        "false_positive": "False Positives",
   140→    }
   141→
   142→    lines: list[str] = [
   143→        "---",
   144→        f"## Skipped ({len(skipped)} items)",
   145→        "",
   146→    ]
   147→
   148→    for kind in ("temporary", "permanent", "false_positive"):
   149→        ids = by_kind.get(kind, [])
   150→        if not ids:
   151→            continue
   152→        lines.append(f"### {kind_labels[kind]} ({len(ids)})")
   153→        lines.append("")
   154→        for finding_id in ids:
   155→            entry = skipped.get(finding_id, {})
   156→            item = by_id.get(finding_id)
   157→            if item:
   158→                lines.extend(render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(finding_id, {})))
   159→            else:
   160→                lines.append(f"- ~~{finding_id}~~ (not in current queue)")
   161→            reason = entry.get("reason")
   162→            if reason:
   163→                lines.append(f"      Reason: {reason}")
   164→            note = entry.get("note")
   165→            if note and not overrides.get(finding_id, {}).get("note"):
   166→                lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")
   167→            review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   168→            if review_after:
   169→                skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   170→                lines.append(f"      Review after: scan {skipped_at + review_after}")
   171→        lines.append("")
   172→    return lines
   173→
   174→
   175→def plan_superseded_section(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   176→    """Render the superseded items section."""
   177→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   178→    if not superseded:
   179→        return []
   180→
   181→    lines: list[str] = [
   182→        "---",
   183→        f"## Superseded ({len(superseded)} items — may need remap)",
   184→        "",
   185→    ]
   186→    for finding_id, entry in superseded.items():
   187→        summary = entry.get("original_summary", "")
   188→        summary_str = f" — {summary}" if summary else ""
   189→        lines.append(f"- ~~{finding_id}~~{summary_str}")
   190→        candidates = entry.get("candidates", [])
   191→        if candidates:
   192→            lines.append(f"  Candidates: {', '.join(candidates[:3])}")
   193→        note = entry.get("note")
   194→        if note:
   195→            lines.append(f"  Note: {note}")
   196→    lines.append("")
   197→    return lines
   198→
   199→
```

    17→# Display ordering preferences for subjective dimensions on the scorecard.

    89→
    90→def _compose_scorecard_dimensions(subjective_order: tuple[str, ...]) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    91→    merged: list[str] = []
    92→    for name in (*_MECHANICAL_SCORECARD_DIMENSIONS, *subjective_order):
    93→        if name not in merged:
    94→            merged.append(name)
    95→    return tuple(merged[:_SCORECARD_MAX_DIMENSIONS])
    96→

   100→    "typescript": _compose_scorecard_dimensions(_SUBJECTIVE_SCORECARD_ORDER_BY_LANG["typescript"]),

Now I have a comprehensive picture. Here is my assessment.

---

## Engine Layer Analysis: Concrete Assessment

### Overall Verdict: The engine layer is getting meaningfully better, with a few areas that need attention.

The changes fall into four major themes: (1) a comprehensive "synthesis" to "triage" rename with richer workflow stages, (2) removal of the tier system in favor of impact-based ranking, (3) extraction of large functions into focused modules, and (4) new capabilities (execution logging, query payload budgeting, promoted IDs). I will walk through each area with specific examples.

---

### 1. `desloppify/engine/_plan/` -- Schema, Operations, Reconcile, Auto-cluster, Stale Dimensions

**Good changes:**

- **Schema migrations extracted to `schema_migrations.py`** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema_migrations.py`): The old `schema.py` was 250+ lines with 5 migration functions mixed in with type definitions. The new file is a clean separation -- types and validation in `schema.py`, migration logic in its own module. The imports use private aliases (`migrate_deferred_to_skipped as _migrate_deferred_to_skipped`) so the public surface of `schema.py` does not change. This is a genuine improvement in module cohesion.

- **New `ExecutionLogEntry` TypedDict** in `schema.py` and the `append_log_entry()` function in `operations.py`: This is a real feature addition. The execution log provides an audit trail for plan mutations, capped at a configurable limit (default 10,000 via `config.py`). The implementation is clean -- the config read is guarded with a try/except to avoid import-time failures, and the cap is enforced with simple list slicing.

- **`promoted_ids` tracking** in `operations.py`: When users explicitly `move_items` to the top, those IDs are tracked in `promoted_ids`. This is then used by `_after_promoted()` in `stale_dimensions.py` to insert auto-generated items (unscored dimensions, triage stages) *after* user-promoted items rather than at position 0. This solves a real UX problem where automated injections would constantly jump ahead of user-prioritized work.

- **`merge_clusters()` in operations.py**: Clean implementation that deduplicates finding IDs, copies missing metadata from source to target, and cleans up override references. No obvious bugs.

- **`_repair_ghost_cluster_refs()` in `auto_cluster.py`**: A self-healing mechanism for orphaned override cluster references. Defensive and appropriate.

- **Triage stage decomposition** in `stale_dimensions.py`: Replacing the single `synthesis::pending` ID with four explicit stage IDs (`triage::observe`, `triage::reflect`, `triage::organize`, `triage::commit`) is architecturally sound. It makes the workflow more transparent and allows partial progress tracking.

**Potential issues:**

- **`_current_unscored_ids` fallback path** in `stale_dimensions.py` (lines 88-120): The original only checked `subjective_assessments`. The new version adds a fallback to `dimension_scores` for pre-review states. This is correctly handling a gap, but the two code paths could return different results for the same state if both dicts have partial data. The `dimension_scores` fallback only looks for the `subjective_assessment` detector key, which should be correct, but the dual-source pattern is fragile.

- **`_current_under_target_ids`** in `stale_dimensions.py` (line 138): This imports from `desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection` inside a function. That module exists, but this creates a circular-ish dependency path: `engine/_plan/stale_dimensions` -> `engine/planning/scorecard_projection`. The original code kept the `_plan` layer independent of `planning`. This import should be verified to not cause import-order issues at module load time. The function-level import defers it, which is good defensive practice.

- **`auto_cluster.py` growth**: The `auto_cluster_findings` function was already long and this diff adds another ~80 lines for the under-target cluster. The function is now handling 4 different cluster types (detector-based, stale, unscored, under-target) with nearly identical boilerplate for each. This is a candidate for further refactoring to extract a common "ensure cluster exists and sync membership" helper.

- **`_current_stale_ids` is now exported in `__all__`** (with underscore prefix). Exporting private-named functions in `__all__` is a minor style inconsistency.

---

### 2. `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/` -- Core, Helpers, Ranking

**Good changes:**

- **`_apply_plan_order` and `_collapse_clusters` extracted to `plan_order.py`** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/plan_order.py`): The old `core.py` was 400+ lines with these two large functions inline. The new `plan_order.py` is 237 lines of focused, well-documented code. The `collapse_clusters` rewrite is particularly improved -- instead of building a `non_cluster_items` list and then sorting at the end, it now walks the list in order and replaces the first member of each cluster with a meta-item. This preserves the sort + plan-order, which is what you want.

- **Removal of subjective interleave guardrail** from `core.py`: The `_apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail` function was an O(n) deque-based interleaver that mixed subjective and objective items. This is removed because the new impact-based sorting makes it unnecessary -- subjective items naturally sort among objective items based on their dimension headroom. This simplifies the queue pipeline significantly.

- **Removal of the tier system**: `tier_counts()`, `choose_fallback_tier()`, `_item_matches_tier()`, the `tier` parameter on `QueueBuildOptions`, `no_tier_fallback`, `selected_tier`, `fallback_reason` in `WorkQueueResult` -- all gone. The old tier system was a crutch that hardcoded priority buckets. The new system uses `estimated_impact` (computed from dimension-level score headroom) as a continuous ranking signal. This is a better model because it accounts for how much a fix will actually improve the overall score.

- **`enrich_with_impact()` in `ranking.py`**: Stamps `estimated_impact` on each item by looking up the dimension's headroom and weight. Clean implementation that falls back to 0.0 when data is missing.

- **`build_triage_stage_items()` replaces `build_synthesis_item()`** in `helpers.py`: Instead of a single monolithic "synthesis needed" item, you get up to 4 stage items with explicit dependency tracking (`blocked_by` lists). This is much more actionable for the user.

**Potential issues:**

- **Removed `sibling_ids` from plan_cluster annotation** in `plan_order.py` line 70: The old `_apply_plan_order` included `"sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])` in the `plan_cluster` annotation. The new version omits it. There is a test in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/commands/test_direct_coverage_modules.py` (line 374) that still expects `sibling_ids`. This will likely be a test failure.

- **`new_item_ids()` uses `first_seen >= threshold`** (line 19 of `plan_order.py`): This is a string comparison of ISO timestamps. This works correctly for ISO 8601 format but is fragile if the format ever changes. It is consistent with the existing codebase pattern though.

- **`_enforce_workflow_stage_order` modifies items in-place** by replacing entries at indices, but only for `workflow_stage` items. If two workflow stages have the same `stage_index` and different `is_blocked` status, the sort is stable. This looks correct.

---

### 3. `desloppify/engine/_scoring/results/core.py` and new health/impact modules

**Good changes:**

- **`compute_health_breakdown` and `compute_health_score` extracted to `health.py`** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/results/health.py`): This is a clean extraction. The old `core.py` was ~500 lines; the health computation was ~130 of those. The new `health.py` is 196 lines of focused code with no behavioral changes.

- **`compute_score_impact` and `get_dimension_for_detector` extracted to `impact.py`** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/results/impact.py`): Also a clean extraction. The `impact.py` module correctly imports `compute_health_score` from `health.py` rather than duplicating it. The old `core.py` now imports and re-exports these, maintaining backward compatibility.

These extractions are textbook module decomposition -- no logic changes, just better organization.

---

### 4. `desloppify/engine/_state/` -- Schema, Filtering, Resolution, Scoring

**Good changes:**

- **`json_default` and score accessors extracted to `schema_scores.py`** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/schema_scores.py`): The original `schema.py` mixed TypedDicts, validation, and utility functions. The new file cleanly separates the JSON/score helper functions. The score accessors in the new file add explicit type checking (`isinstance(value, int | float)`) that the originals lacked -- this is a hardening improvement.

- **`_matches_pattern` enhancement in `filtering.py`**: The pattern matching now supports hex-suffix matching (`_HEX8_RE.match(pattern) and finding_id.endswith("::" + pattern)`) and name-segment fallback for bare names. This improves UX significantly -- users can now specify a finding by just its trailing segment instead of the full ID. The implementation is clean, with the regex pre-compiled at module level.

- **`matched_ignore_pattern` fix in `filtering.py`**: The old code did exact `file == pattern` matching. The new code properly handles directory patterns: `file.startswith(base + "/")` catches files inside ignored directories. This fixes a real bug where ignoring `foo/bar/` would not catch `foo/bar/baz.py`.

- **Defensive backfill in `scoring.py`**: Adding `carried.setdefault("score", 0.0)` and similar for `strict`/`strict_score` in `_update_objective_health` prevents KeyError when carrying forward dimension data from older state files.

**Potential issue:**

- **Resolution attestation workaround** in `resolution.py` (lines 134-139): The original code built the dict inline. The new code builds it in two steps, setting `"previous_status"` separately via `reopen_attestation["previous_status"] = previous_status`. This pattern looks like it is working around a TypedDict or type-checker complaint. The behavior is identical, but it is a slightly odd indirection.

---

### 5. `desloppify/engine/plan.py` and `desloppify/engine/work_queue.py`

These are facade modules. The changes here are purely mechanical: updating imports and `__all__` to reflect the synthesis-to-triage rename and new exports. No logic, no risk. The facade pattern is well maintained -- all internal module changes are properly re-exported.

---

### 6. `desloppify/engine/planning/` -- Render, Common, Core, Select, Queue Policy

**Good changes:**

- **`render_sections.py`** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/render_sections.py`): Extracts `_addressed_section`, `_plan_skipped_section`, `_plan_superseded_section`, `_plan_user_ordered_section`, `_render_plan_item`, and `_tier_summary_lines` from `render.py`. The old `render.py` was enormous. The extracted code is identical with one improvement: `_render_plan_item` (renamed to `render_plan_item`) drops the `[T{tier}/...]` badge from plan items, which is consistent with the tier removal.

- **`_plan_tier_sections` renamed to `_plan_item_sections`** and simplified: No longer groups by tier -- just groups by file. Removes ~100 lines of tier-specific rendering logic.

- **`scorecard_policy.py`** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/scorecard_policy.py`): Contains dimension ordering constants for the scorecard display. This was likely inline or scattered before. Clean data-only module.

- **`triage.py`** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/triage.py`): A thin re-export module that surfaces `TriageInput`, `build_triage_prompt`, etc. from `engine/_plan/epic_triage`. This avoids app-layer code importing from the private `_plan` package directly. Good layering.

- **`TIER_LABELS` removed from `common.py`**: Consistent with the tier removal throughout.

---

### 7. `desloppify/core/` -- Query, Config, Grep

**Good changes:**

- **Query payload budgeting in `query.py`**: This is a substantial and well-designed addition. The old `write_query` would dump arbitrarily large JSON to `query.json`. The new system:
  - Defines a 2MB budget (`QUERY_PAYLOAD_MAX_BYTES`)
  - Applies progressive truncation: limit items to 200, truncate text fields, compact detail dicts, collapse cluster members, then fall back to a minimal skeleton
  - Each truncation step checks size before proceeding
  - A `query_truncated` metadata dict records what was done
  
  This is defensive engineering for a real problem (large codebases producing huge query payloads).

- **`config.py`**: Single-line addition of `execution_log_max_entries` config key. Clean.

- **`grep.py` delegation**: `grep_files_containing` now delegates to `desloppify.search.grep_files_containing`. This deduplicates the regex-building and file-scanning logic that was duplicated between the two modules. Clean refactoring.

**Potential issue:**

- **`_fit_payload_to_budget` halving loop** in `query.py` (lines 106-109): The loop `while keep > 0 and _payload_size_bytes(trimmed) > max_bytes: keep = max(0, keep // 2)` recomputes JSON serialization on every iteration. For a 200-item list, this is at most 8 iterations (200 -> 100 -> 50 -> ... -> 0), so it is not a performance concern. But `_payload_size_bytes` calls `json.dumps` on the entire payload each time, not just the items. This could be improved but is unlikely to matter in practice.

---

### 8. New Module: `desloppify/engine/hook_registry.py`

This is a scope-aware registry for language hook modules (currently only `test_coverage`). It uses `ScopedDictStore` for isolation, supports lazy loading via `importlib`, and has proper scope context management. This appears to be a genuine new capability for multi-project or test-isolated scenarios. The code is clean and well-structured with appropriate error handling on all import/reload operations.

---

### Summary: Specific Good and Bad

**Best changes (genuinely improving the codebase):**

1. Schema migrations extracted to `schema_migrations.py` -- clean separation of concerns
2. Health/impact scoring extracted from the 500-line `core.py` -- textbook decomposition
3. `_apply_plan_order` and `_collapse_clusters` moved to `plan_order.py` with improved collapse algorithm
4. Tier system replaced with continuous impact-based ranking -- architecturally better model
5. Query payload budgeting with progressive truncation -- defensive engineering for a real problem
6. `promoted_ids` preventing auto-injected items from trampling user priorities
7. Directory matching fix in `matched_ignore_pattern` -- genuine bug fix
8. Pattern matching improvements in `_matches_pattern` -- real UX improvement

**Areas of concern:**

1. **Likely test breakage**: `sibling_ids` removed from `plan_cluster` annotation in `plan_order.py` but still expected in test `test_direct_coverage_modules.py`
2. **`auto_cluster_findings` growing too large**: Now handles 4 cluster types with repetitive boilerplate; needs a common helper
3. **`_current_stale_ids` exported with underscore prefix** in `__all__` -- style inconsistency
4. **Cross-layer import from `_plan` into `planning`** (`stale_dimensions.py` importing `scorecard_projection`): works due to function-level deferral but adds an unusual dependency direction
5. **`epic_triage.py` rename is purely mechanical** -- the "triage" name is better than "synthesis" but the entire file is a straightforward find-and-replace with zero logic changes; this is fine but adds rename-churn to the diff

**Verdict: The engine layer is getting better.** The refactoring is extracting logic into genuinely cleaner modules with better separation of concerns. The tier-to-impact transition is an architectural improvement. The new capabilities (execution log, query budgeting, promoted IDs, multi-stage triage) are well-implemented. The main risk is the `sibling_ids` removal causing downstream test failures, and the `auto_cluster_findings` function needs a second pass to reduce its size.

+
+
+def _resolve_target(plan: dict, target: str | None, position: str) -> str | None:
+    """Resolve a cluster name used as a before/after target to a member ID."""
+    if target is None:
+        return None
+    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
+    if target not in clusters:
+        return target
+    member_ids = clusters[target].get("finding_ids", [])
+    if not member_ids:
+        return target
+    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
+    member_set = set(member_ids)
+    ordered = [fid for fid in order if fid in member_set]
+    if not ordered:
+        return member_ids[0]
+    return ordered[0] if position == "before" else ordered[-1]
 
 
 def cmd_plan_move(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

+        return
