1→"""Python import-resolution policy helpers used by dependency detectors."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.text_api import PROJECT_ROOT
     8→
     9→
    10→def resolve_python_from_import(
    11→    module_path: str,
    12→    import_names: str,
    13→    source_file: str,
    14→    scan_root: Path,
    15→) -> list[str]:
    16→    """Resolve a ``from X import Y`` statement to one or more file paths."""
    17→    source = (
    18→        Path(source_file)
    19→        if Path(source_file).is_absolute()
    20→        else PROJECT_ROOT / source_file
    21→    )
    22→    source_dir = source.parent
    23→    scan_root_path = Path(scan_root) if not isinstance(scan_root, Path) else scan_root
    24→
    25→    dots_only = all(ch == "." for ch in module_path)
    26→    if dots_only:
    27→        dots = len(module_path)
    28→        base = source_dir
    29→        for _ in range(dots - 1):
    30→            base = base.parent
    31→
    32→        results: list[str] = []
    33→        names = [name.strip().split()[0] for name in import_names.split(",")]
    34→        for name in names:
    35→            if not name or name.startswith("(") or name.startswith("#"):
    36→                continue
    37→            cleaned = name.strip("()")
    38→            if not cleaned:
    39→                continue
    40→            target = try_resolve_path(base / cleaned)
    41→            if target:
    42→                results.append(target)
    43→
    44→        if not results:
    45→            target = try_resolve_path(base)
    46→            if target:
    47→                results.append(target)
    48→        return results
    49→
    50→    results = []
    51→    target = resolve_python_import(module_path, source_file, scan_root_path)
    52→    if target and import_names:
    53→        names = [name.strip().split()[0] for name in import_names.split(",")]
    54→        for name in names:
    55→            cleaned = name.strip("()")
    56→            if not cleaned:
    57→                continue
    58→            submodule = resolve_python_import(
    59→                f"{module_path}.{cleaned}",
    60→                source_file,
    61→                scan_root_path,
    62→            )
    63→            if submodule:
    64→                results.append(submodule)
    65→    if target:
    66→        results.append(target)
    67→    return results
    68→
    69→
    70→def resolve_python_import(
    71→    module_path: str,
    72→    source_file: str,
    73→    scan_root: Path,
    74→) -> str | None:
    75→    """Resolve a Python import module path to a project file."""
    76→    source = (
    77→        Path(source_file)
    78→        if Path(source_file).is_absolute()
    79→        else PROJECT_ROOT / source_file
    80→    )
    81→    source_dir = source.parent
    82→    scan_root_path = Path(scan_root) if not isinstance(scan_root, Path) else scan_root
    83→    if module_path.startswith("."):
    84→        return resolve_relative_import(module_path, source_dir)
    85→    return resolve_absolute_import(module_path, scan_root_path)
    86→
    87→
    88→def resolve_relative_import(module_path: str, source_dir: Path) -> str | None:
    89→    """Resolve a relative import path starting from the source file directory."""
    90→    dots = 0
    91→    for ch in module_path:
    92→        if ch == ".":
    93→            dots += 1
    94→        else:
    95→            break
    96→    remainder = module_path[dots:]
    97→
    98→    base = source_dir
    99→    for _ in range(dots - 1):
   100→        base = base.parent
   101→
   102→    target_base = base
   103→    if remainder:
   104→        for part in remainder.split("."):
   105→            target_base = target_base / part
   106→    return try_resolve_path(target_base)
   107→
   108→
   109→def resolve_absolute_import(module_path: str, scan_root: Path) -> str | None:
   110→    """Resolve an absolute import within scan root first, then project root."""
   111→    parts = module_path.split(".")
   112→    target_base = scan_root.resolve()
   113→    for part in parts:
   114→        target_base = target_base / part
   115→    resolved = try_resolve_path(target_base)
   116→    if resolved:
   117→        return resolved
   118→
   119→    target_base = PROJECT_ROOT
   120→    for part in parts:
   121→        target_base = target_base / part
   122→    return try_resolve_path(target_base)
   123→
   124→
   125→def try_resolve_path(target_base: Path) -> str | None:
   126→    """Try to resolve module base to ``.py`` or package ``__init__.py`` path."""
   127→    candidate = Path(str(target_base) + ".py")
   128→    if candidate.is_file():
   129→        return str(candidate.resolve())
   130→
   131→    candidate = target_base / "__init__.py"
   132→    if candidate.is_file():
   133→        return str(candidate.resolve())
   134→
   135→    if target_base.is_dir():
   136→        init_path = target_base / "__init__.py"
   137→        if init_path.is_file():
   138→            return str(init_path.resolve())
   139→
   140→    return None
   141→
   142→
   143→__all__ = [
   144→    "resolve_absolute_import",
   145→    "resolve_python_from_import",
   146→    "resolve_python_import",
   147→    "resolve_relative_import",
   148→    "try_resolve_path",
   149→]
   150→

1→"""Dynamic import discovery for Python dependency hints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import ast
     6→import logging
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from .deps_resolution import resolve_absolute_import
    10→
    11→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    12→
    13→
    14→def find_python_dynamic_imports(path: Path, extensions: list[str]) -> set[str]:
    15→    """Find module specifiers referenced by ``importlib.import_module`` calls."""
    16→    del extensions
    17→    targets: set[str] = set()
    18→    for py_file in path.rglob("*.py"):
    19→        try:
    20→            tree = ast.parse(py_file.read_text(), filename=str(py_file))
    21→        except (SyntaxError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as exc:
    22→            logger.debug(
    23→                "Skipping unreadable file %s in dynamic import scan: %s",
    24→                py_file,
    25→                exc,
    26→            )
    27→            continue
    28→        for node in ast.walk(tree):
    29→            if not isinstance(node, ast.Call):
    30→                continue
    31→            func = node.func
    32→            if not (
    33→                isinstance(func, ast.Attribute)
    34→                and func.attr == "import_module"
    35→                and isinstance(func.value, ast.Name)
    36→                and func.value.id == "importlib"
    37→                and node.args
    38→                and isinstance(node.args[0], ast.Constant)
    39→                and isinstance(node.args[0].value, str)
    40→            ):
    41→                continue
    42→
    43→            spec = node.args[0].value
    44→            resolved = resolve_absolute_import(spec, path)
    45→            if resolved:
    46→                targets.add(resolved)
    47→            else:
    48→                targets.add(spec)
    49→    return targets
    50→
    51→
    52→__all__ = ["find_python_dynamic_imports"]
    53→
```

    37→                        backslashes += 1
    38→                        j -= 1
    39→                    if backslashes % 2 == 0:
    40→                        in_multiline = None
    41→                        break
    42→                    pos = idx + 3
    43→            continue
    44→
    45→        pos = 0
    46→        while pos < len(line):
    47→            ch = line[pos]
    48→            if ch == "#":
    49→                break
    50→            if ch in ("r", "b", "f", "u", "R", "B", "F", "U") and pos + 1 < len(line):
    51→                next_ch = line[pos + 1]
    52→                if next_ch in ('"', "'"):
    53→                    pos += 1
    54→                    ch = next_ch
    55→                elif (
    56→                    next_ch in ("r", "b", "f", "R", "B", "F")
    57→                    and pos + 2 < len(line)
    58→                    and line[pos + 2] in ('"', "'")
    59→                ):
    60→                    pos += 2
    61→                    ch = line[pos]
    62→            if ch in ('"', "'"):
    63→                triple = line[pos : pos + 3]
    64→                if triple in ('"""', "'''"):
    65→                    close_idx = line.find(triple, pos + 3)
    66→                    if close_idx == -1:
    67→                        in_multiline = triple
    68→                        break
    69→                    pos = close_idx + 3
    70→                    continue
    71→                end = line.find(ch, pos + 1)
    72→                while end != -1 and end > 0 and line[end - 1] == "\\":
    73→                    end = line.find(ch, end + 1)
    74→                pos = (end + 1) if end != -1 else len(line)
    75→                continue
    76→            pos += 1
    77→
    78→    return string_lines
    79→
    80→
    81→def _match_is_in_string(line: str, match_start: int) -> bool:
    82→    """Return True when a regex match location is inside a string/comment."""
    83→    i = 0
    84→    in_string = None
    85→    while i < len(line):
    86→        if i == match_start:
    87→            return in_string is not None
    88→        ch = line[i]
    89→        if in_string is None:
    90→            if ch == "#":
    91→                return True
    92→            triple = line[i : i + 3]
    93→            if triple in ('"""', "'''"):
    94→                in_string = triple
    95→                i += 3
    96→                continue
    97→            if (
    98→                ch in ("r", "b", "f")
    99→                and i + 1 < len(line)
   100→                and line[i + 1] in ('"', "'")
   101→            ):
   102→                i += 1
   103→                ch = line[i]
   104→            if ch in ('"', "'"):
   105→                in_string = ch
   106→                i += 1
   107→                continue
   108→        else:
   109→            if ch == "\\" and i + 1 < len(line):
   110→                i += 2
   111→                continue
   112→            if in_string in ('"""', "'''"):
   113→                if line[i : i + 3] == in_string:
   114→                    in_string = None
   115→                    i += 3
   116→                    continue
   117→            elif ch == in_string:
   118→                in_string = None
   119→                i += 1
   120→                continue
   121→        i += 1
   122→    return in_string is not None
   123→
   124→
   125→def _walk_except_blocks(lines: list[str]):
   126→    """Yield ``(line_index, except_clause, body_lines)`` for except blocks."""
   127→    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
   128→        stripped = line.strip()
   129→        if not re.match(r"except\s*(?:\w|\(|:)", stripped) and stripped != "except:":
   130→            continue
   131→        if not stripped.endswith(":"):
   132→            continue
   133→        indent = len(line) - len(line.lstrip())
   134→        j = i + 1
   135→        body_lines = []
   136→        while j < len(lines):
   137→            next_line = lines[j]
   138→            next_stripped = next_line.strip()
   139→            if next_stripped == "":
   140→                j += 1
   141→                continue
   142→            if len(next_line) - len(next_line.lstrip()) <= indent:
   143→                break
   144→            body_lines.append(next_stripped)
   145→            j += 1
   146→        yield i, stripped, body_lines
   147→
   148→
   149→def _is_broad_except(stripped: str) -> bool:
   150→    """Check if except clause catches broadly (bare, Exception, BaseException)."""
   151→    if stripped == "except:":
   152→        return True
   153→    match = re.match(r"except\s+(\w+)", stripped)
   154→    return bool(match and match.group(1) in ("Exception", "BaseException"))
   155→
   156→
   157→def _detect_empty_except(filepath: str, lines: list[str], smell_counts: dict[str, list]) -> None:
   158→    """Find broad except blocks that just pass or have empty body."""
   159→    for i, stripped, body_lines in _walk_except_blocks(lines):
   160→        if (not body_lines or body_lines == ["pass"]) and _is_broad_except(stripped):
   161→            smell_counts["empty_except"].append(
   162→                {
   163→                    "file": filepath,
   164→                    "line": i + 1,
   165→                    "content": stripped[:100],
   166→                }
   167→            )
   168→
   169→
   170→def _detect_swallowed_errors(
   171→    filepath: str,
   172→    lines: list[str],
   173→    smell_counts: dict[str, list],
   174→) -> None:
   175→    """Find except blocks that only print/log the error."""
   176→    log_re = r"(?:print|logging\.\w+|logger\.\w+|log\.\w+)\s*\("
   177→    for i, stripped, body_lines in _walk_except_blocks(lines):
   178→        if body_lines and all(re.match(log_re, statement) for statement in body_lines):
   179→            smell_counts["swallowed_error"].append(
   180→                {
   181→                    "file": filepath,
   182→                    "line": i + 1,
   183→                    "content": stripped[:100],
   184→                }
   185→            )
   186→
   187→
   188→def detect_smells(
   189→    path: Path,
   190→    *,
   191→    smell_checks: list[dict],
   192→    is_test_path_fn,
   193→    logger,
   194→) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
   195→    """Detect Python code smell patterns. Returns (entries, total_files_checked)."""
   196→    smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]] = {smell["id"]: [] for smell in smell_checks}
   197→    files = find_py_files(path)
   198→    constants_by_key: dict[tuple[str, str], list[tuple[str, int]]] = {}
   199→
   200→    for filepath in files:
   201→        try:
   202→            file_path = (
   203→                Path(filepath) if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else PROJECT_ROOT / filepath
   204→            )
   205→            content = file_path.read_text()
   206→            lines = content.splitlines()
   207→        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
   208→            log_best_effort_failure(
   209→                logger,
   210→                f"read Python file for smell scan {filepath}",
   211→                exc,
   212→            )
   213→            continue
   214→
   215→        if is_test_path_fn(filepath):
   216→            continue
   217→
   218→        multiline_string_lines = _build_string_line_set(lines)
   219→
   220→        for check in smell_checks:
   221→            pattern = check.get("pattern")
   222→            if pattern is None:
   223→                continue
   224→            for i, line in enumerate(lines):
   225→                if i in multiline_string_lines:
   226→                    continue
   227→                match = re.search(pattern, line)
   228→                if match and not _match_is_in_string(line, match.start()):
   229→                    if check["id"] == "hardcoded_url" and re.match(
   230→                        r"^[A-Z_][A-Z0-9_]*\s*=",
   231→                        line.strip(),
   232→                    ):
   233→                        continue
   234→                    smell_counts[check["id"]].append(
   235→                        {
   236→                            "file": filepath,
   237→                            "line": i + 1,
   238→                            "content": line.strip()[:100],
   239→                        }
   240→                    )
   241→
   242→        _detect_empty_except(filepath, lines, smell_counts)
   243→        _detect_swallowed_errors(filepath, lines, smell_counts)
   244→        detect_ast_smells(filepath, content, smell_counts)
   245→        detect_star_import_no_all(filepath, content, path, smell_counts)
   246→        detect_vestigial_parameter(filepath, content, lines, smell_counts)
   247→        collect_module_constants(filepath, content, constants_by_key)
   248→
   249→    detect_duplicate_constants(constants_by_key, smell_counts)
   250→
   251→    severity_order = {"high": 0, "medium": 1, "low": 2}
   252→    entries = []
   253→    for check in smell_checks:
   254→        matches = smell_counts[check["id"]]
   255→        if matches:
   256→            entries.append(
   257→                {
   258→                    "id": check["id"],
   259→                    "label": check["label"],
   260→                    "severity": check["severity"],
   261→                    "count": len(matches),
   262→                    "files": len(set(match["file"] for match in matches)),
   263→                    "matches": matches[:50],
   264→                }
   265→            )
   266→    entries.sort(
   267→        key=lambda entry: (severity_order.get(entry["severity"], 9), -entry["count"])
   268→    )
   269→    return entries, len(files)
   270→
   271→
   272→__all__ = ["detect_smells"]
   273→
```

1→"""Resolution helpers for TypeScript dependency graph extraction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→from functools import lru_cache
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→from typing import Any
    10→
    11→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    12→
    13→_RESOLVE_EXTENSIONS = ("", ".ts", ".tsx", "/index.ts", "/index.tsx")
    14→_JS_SPECIFIER_EXTENSIONS = {".js", ".mjs", ".cjs"}
    15→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    16→
    17→
    18→@lru_cache(maxsize=32)
    19→def load_tsconfig_paths_cached(project_root_str: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    20→    """Return cached tsconfig path mappings for a project root."""
    21→    return parse_tsconfig_paths(Path(project_root_str))
    22→
    23→
    24→def load_tsconfig_paths(project_root: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    25→    """Parse tsconfig.json compilerOptions.paths into alias-to-directory mappings."""
    26→    return load_tsconfig_paths_cached(str(project_root.resolve()))
    27→
    28→
    29→def parse_tsconfig_paths(project_root: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    30→    """Parse tsconfig paths from disk. Internal — use ``load_tsconfig_paths``."""
    31→    fallback = {"@/": "src/"}
    32→
    33→    for name in ("tsconfig.json", "tsconfig.app.json", "jsconfig.json"):
    34→        config_path = project_root / name
    35→        if not config_path.is_file():
    36→            continue
    37→        try:
    38→            data = json.loads(config_path.read_text(errors="replace"))
    39→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError) as exc:
    40→            log_best_effort_failure(
    41→                logger,
    42→                f"parse TypeScript config file {config_path}",
    43→                exc,
    44→            )
    45→            continue
    46→        result = extract_paths(data, project_root)
    47→        if result is not None:
    48→            return result
    49→        extends = data.get("extends")
    50→        if isinstance(extends, str) and not extends.startswith("@"):
    51→            parent_path = (config_path.parent / extends).resolve()
    52→            if parent_path.is_file():
    53→                try:
    54→                    parent_data = json.loads(parent_path.read_text(errors="replace"))
    55→                except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
    56→                    return fallback
    57→                parent_result = extract_paths(parent_data, parent_path.parent)
    58→                if parent_result is not None:
    59→                    return parent_result
    60→        return fallback
    61→
    62→    return fallback
    63→
    64→
    65→def extract_paths(data: dict, base_dir: Path) -> dict[str, str] | None:
    66→    """Extract paths mapping from a parsed tsconfig."""
    67→    del base_dir
    68→    compiler_options = data.get("compilerOptions")
    69→    if not isinstance(compiler_options, dict):
    70→        return None
    71→    paths = compiler_options.get("paths")
    72→    if not isinstance(paths, dict):
    73→        return None
    74→
    75→    base_url = compiler_options.get("baseUrl", ".")
    76→    if not isinstance(base_url, str):
    77→        base_url = "."
    78→
    79→    result: dict[str, str] = {}
    80→    for alias, targets in paths.items():
    81→        if not isinstance(targets, list) or not targets:
    82→            continue
    83→        target = targets[0]
    84→        if not isinstance(target, str):
    85→            continue
    86→        alias_prefix = alias.removesuffix("*")
    87→        target_prefix = target.removesuffix("*")
    88→        target_dir = target_prefix.removeprefix("./")
    89→        if base_url != ".":
    90→            base = base_url.rstrip("/")
    91→            target_dir = base + "/" + target_dir if target_dir else base + "/"
    92→        result[alias_prefix] = target_dir
    93→    return result if result else None
    94→
    95→
    96→def iter_resolve_candidates(target: Path):
    97→    """Yield filesystem candidates for a module specifier target."""
    98→    seen: set[str] = set()
    99→
   100→    def _emit(candidate: Path):
   101→        key = str(candidate)
   102→        if key in seen:
   103→            return
   104→        seen.add(key)
   105→        yield candidate
   106→
   107→    if target.suffix in {".ts", ".tsx"}:
   108→        yield from _emit(target)
   109→        return
   110→
   111→    if target.suffix in _JS_SPECIFIER_EXTENSIONS:
   112→        stem = target.with_suffix("")
   113→        yield from _emit(Path(str(stem) + ".ts"))
   114→        yield from _emit(Path(str(stem) + ".tsx"))
   115→        yield from _emit(Path(str(stem) + "/index.ts"))
   116→        yield from _emit(Path(str(stem) + "/index.tsx"))
   117→        yield from _emit(target)
   118→        return
   119→
   120→    for ext in _RESOLVE_EXTENSIONS:
   121→        yield from _emit(Path(str(target) + ext))
   122→
   123→
   124→def resolve_alias(
   125→    module_path: str,
   126→    tsconfig_paths: dict[str, str],
   127→    project_root: Path,
   128→) -> Path | None:
   129→    """Resolve a tsconfig path alias to an absolute path."""
   130→    for prefix, target_dir in tsconfig_paths.items():
   131→        if module_path.startswith(prefix):
   132→            relative = module_path[len(prefix) :]
   133→            return (project_root / target_dir / relative).resolve()
   134→    return None
   135→
   136→
   137→def resolve_module(
   138→    module_path: str,
   139→    filepath: str,
   140→    tsconfig_paths: dict[str, str],
   141→    project_root: Path,
   142→    graph: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
   143→    source_resolved: str,
   144→) -> None:
   145→    """Resolve an import specifier and add edges to the graph."""
   146→    target: Path | None = None
   147→    if module_path.startswith("."):
   148→        source_dir = (
   149→            Path(filepath).parent
   150→            if Path(filepath).is_absolute()
   151→            else (project_root / filepath).parent
   152→        )
   153→        target = (source_dir / module_path).resolve()
   154→    else:
   155→        target = resolve_alias(module_path, tsconfig_paths, project_root)
   156→
   157→    if target is None:
   158→        return
   159→
   160→    for candidate in iter_resolve_candidates(target):
   161→        if candidate.is_file():
   162→            target_resolved = str(candidate)
   163→            graph[source_resolved]["imports"].add(target_resolved)
   164→            graph[target_resolved]["importers"].add(source_resolved)
   165→            break
   166→

1→"""Extracted heavy phase implementations for Python language pipeline."""

    10→from desloppify.engine.detectors import gods as gods_detector_mod
    11→from desloppify.engine.detectors import graph as graph_detector_mod

    35→from desloppify.languages.python.extractors_classes import extract_py_classes

    38→
    39→def run_phase_structural(
    40→    path: Path,
    41→    lang: LangRun,
    42→    *,
    43→    complexity_signals: list[ComplexitySignal],
    44→    god_rules: list[GodRule],
    45→    log_fn,
    46→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    47→    """Merge large + complexity + god classes into structural findings."""
    48→    structural: dict[str, dict] = {}
    49→
    50→    large_entries, file_count = large_detector_mod.detect_large_files(
    51→        path, file_finder=lang.file_finder, threshold=lang.large_threshold
    52→    )
    53→    for entry in large_entries:
    54→        add_structural_signal(
    55→            structural,
    56→            entry["file"],
    57→            f"large ({entry['loc']} LOC)",
    58→            {"loc": entry["loc"]},
    59→        )
    60→
    61→    complexity_entries, _ = complexity_detector_mod.detect_complexity(
    62→        path,
    63→        signals=complexity_signals,
    64→        file_finder=lang.file_finder,
    65→        threshold=lang.complexity_threshold,
    66→    )
    67→    for entry in complexity_entries:
    68→        add_structural_signal(
    69→            structural,
    70→            entry["file"],
    71→            f"complexity score {entry['score']}",
    72→            {"complexity_score": entry["score"], "complexity_signals": entry["signals"]},
    73→        )
    74→        lang.complexity_map[entry["file"]] = entry["score"]
    75→
    76→    god_entries, _ = gods_detector_mod.detect_gods(extract_py_classes(path), god_rules)
    77→    for entry in god_entries:
    78→        add_structural_signal(
    79→            structural,
    80→            entry["file"],
    81→            entry["signal_text"],
    82→            entry["detail"],
    83→        )
    84→
    85→    results = merge_structural_signals(structural, log_fn)
    86→
    87→    flat_entries, dir_count = flat_dirs_detector_mod.detect_flat_dirs(
    88→        path, file_finder=lang.file_finder
    89→    )
    90→    for entry in flat_entries:
    91→        child_dir_count = int(entry.get("child_dir_count", 0))
    92→        combined_score = int(entry.get("combined_score", entry.get("file_count", 0)))
    93→        results.append(
    94→            state_mod.make_finding(
    95→                "flat_dirs",
    96→                entry["directory"],
    97→                "",
    98→                tier=3,
    99→                confidence="medium",
   100→                summary=flat_dirs_detector_mod.format_flat_dir_summary(entry),
   101→                detail={
   102→                    "file_count": entry["file_count"],
   103→                    "child_dir_count": child_dir_count,
   104→                    "combined_score": combined_score,
   105→                    "kind": entry.get("kind", "overload"),
   106→                    "parent_sibling_count": int(entry.get("parent_sibling_count", 0)),
   107→                    "wrapper_item_count": int(entry.get("wrapper_item_count", 0)),
   108→                    "sparse_child_count": int(entry.get("sparse_child_count", 0)),
   109→                    "sparse_child_ratio": float(entry.get("sparse_child_ratio", 0.0)),
   110→                    "sparse_child_file_threshold": int(
   111→                        entry.get("sparse_child_file_threshold", 0)
   112→                    ),
   113→                },
   114→            )
   115→        )
   116→    if flat_entries:
   117→        log_fn(
   118→            f"         flat dirs: {len(flat_entries)} overloaded directories "
   119→            "(files/subdirs/combined)"
   120→        )
   121→
   122→    passthrough_entries = detect_passthrough_functions(path)
   123→    results.extend(
   124→        make_passthrough_findings(
   125→            passthrough_entries,
   126→            "function",
   127→            "total_params",
   128→            log_fn,
   129→        )
   130→    )
   131→
   132→    potentials = {
   133→        "structural": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, file_count),
   134→        "flat_dirs": dir_count,
   135→        "props": len(passthrough_entries) if passthrough_entries else 0,
   136→    }
   137→    return results, potentials
   138→
   139→
   140→def run_phase_coupling(path: Path, lang: LangRun, *, log_fn) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   141→    """Run coupling-related detectors and return findings/potentials."""
   142→    graph = deps_detector_mod.build_dep_graph(path)
   143→    lang.dep_graph = graph
   144→    zone_map = lang.zone_map
   145→
   146→    single_entries, single_candidates = (
   147→        single_use_detector_mod.detect_single_use_abstractions(
   148→            path, graph, barrel_names=lang.barrel_names
   149→        )
   150→    )
   151→    single_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, single_entries, "single_use")
   152→    results = make_single_use_findings(
   153→        single_entries, lang.get_area, skip_dir_names={"commands"}, stderr_fn=log_fn
   154→    )
   155→
   156→    cycle_entries, _ = graph_detector_mod.detect_cycles(graph)
   157→    cycle_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, cycle_entries, "cycles", file_key="files")
   158→    results.extend(make_cycle_findings(cycle_entries, log_fn))
   159→
   160→    orphan_entries, total_graph_files = orphaned_detector_mod.detect_orphaned_files(
   161→        path,
   162→        graph,
   163→        extensions=lang.extensions,
   164→        options=orphaned_detector_mod.OrphanedDetectionOptions(
   165→            extra_entry_patterns=lang.entry_patterns,
   166→            extra_barrel_names=lang.barrel_names,
   167→            dynamic_import_finder=deps_detector_mod.find_python_dynamic_imports,
   168→        ),
   169→    )
   170→    orphan_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, orphan_entries, "orphaned")
   171→    results.extend(make_orphaned_findings(orphan_entries, log_fn))
   172→
   173→    facade_entries, _ = facade_detector_mod.detect_reexport_facades(graph)
   174→    facade_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, facade_entries, "facade")
   175→    results.extend(make_facade_findings(facade_entries, log_fn))
   176→
   177→    mixin_entries, coupling_candidates = (
   178→        coupling_contracts_detector_mod.detect_implicit_mixin_contracts(path)
   179→    )
   180→    mixin_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, mixin_entries, "coupling")
   181→    for entry in mixin_entries:
   182→        attr_preview = ", ".join(entry["required_attrs"][:4])
   183→        if len(entry["required_attrs"]) > 4:
   184→            attr_preview += f", +{len(entry['required_attrs']) - 4} more"
   185→        required_count = entry["required_count"]
   186→        tier = 4 if required_count >= 6 else 3
   187→        confidence = "high" if required_count >= 6 else "medium"
   188→        results.append(
   189→            state_mod.make_finding(
   190→                "coupling",
   191→                entry["file"],
   192→                entry["class"],
   193→                tier=tier,
   194→                confidence=confidence,
   195→                summary=(
   196→                    f"Implicit host contract: {entry['class']} depends on {required_count} "
   197→                    f"undeclared self attrs ({attr_preview})"
   198→                ),
   199→                detail={
   200→                    "subtype": "implicit_mixin_contract",
   201→                    "required_attrs": entry["required_attrs"],
   202→                    "required_count": required_count,
   203→                    "line": entry.get("line"),
   204→                },
   205→            )
   206→        )
   207→
   208→    log_fn(f"         -> {len(results)} coupling/structural findings total")
   209→    potentials = {
   210→        "single_use": adjust_potential(zone_map, single_candidates),
   211→        "cycles": adjust_potential(zone_map, total_graph_files),
   212→        "orphaned": adjust_potential(zone_map, total_graph_files),
   213→        "facade": adjust_potential(zone_map, total_graph_files),
   214→        "coupling": adjust_potential(zone_map, coupling_candidates),
   215→    }
   216→    return results, potentials
   217→
   218→
   219→__all__ = ["run_phase_coupling", "run_phase_structural"]
   220→

     }
 
 
+def make_standard_detect_registry_getter(
+    *,
+    cmd_deps: Callable[[argparse.Namespace], None],
+    cmd_cycles: Callable[[argparse.Namespace], None],
+    cmd_orphaned: Callable[[argparse.Namespace], None],
+    cmd_dupes: Callable[[argparse.Namespace], None],
+    cmd_large: Callable[[argparse.Namespace], None],
+    cmd_complexity: Callable[[argparse.Namespace], None],
+) -> Callable[[], dict[str, Callable[[argparse.Namespace], None]]]:
+    """Return a ``get_detect_commands`` callable backed by shared registry logic."""
+
+    def get_detect_commands() -> dict[str, Callable[[argparse.Namespace], None]]:
+        return build_standard_detect_registry(
+            cmd_deps=cmd_deps,
+            cmd_cycles=cmd_cycles,
+            cmd_orphaned=cmd_orphaned,
+            cmd_dupes=cmd_dupes,
+            cmd_large=cmd_large,
+            cmd_complexity=cmd_complexity,
+        )
+
+    return get_detect_commands
+
+

diff --git a/desloppify/languages/go/test_coverage.py b/desloppify/languages/go/test_coverage.py
index 6e4dbe9..e456ee4 100644
--- a/desloppify/languages/go/test_coverage.py
+++ b/desloppify/languages/go/test_coverage.py
@@ -5,6 +5,7 @@ Originally contributed by tinker495 (KyuSeok Jung) in PR #128.
 
 from __future__ import annotations
 
+import os
 import re
 
 ASSERT_PATTERNS = [
@@ -39,7 +40,44 @@ def has_testable_logic(_filepath: str, content: str) -> bool:
 def resolve_import_spec(
     spec: str, _test_path: str, production_files: set[str]
 ) -> str | None:
-    """Go tests use same-package layout, not import spec resolution."""
+    """Best-effort Go import-path to source-file resolution for direct imports."""
+    normalized = spec.strip().strip("\"'`").replace("\\", "/").strip("/")
+    if not normalized or normalized in {"C", "unsafe"}:
+        return None
+
+    segments = [segment for segment in normalized.split("/") if segment]
+    if not segments:
+        return None
+
+    candidates: list[str] = []
+    for idx in range(len(segments)):
+        tail = "/".join(segments[idx:])
+        if not tail:
+            continue
+        leaf = tail.split("/")[-1]
+        candidates.append(f"{tail}.go")
+        candidates.append(f"{tail}/{leaf}.go")
+
+    test_path = _test_path.replace("\\", "/").strip()
+    if test_path:
+        test_dir = os.path.dirname(test_path)
+        leaf = segments[-1]
+        candidates.append(f"{test_dir}/{leaf}.go")
+        parent = os.path.dirname(test_dir)
+        if parent:
+            candidates.append(f"{parent}/{leaf}.go")
+
+    normalized_production = {
+        file_path.replace("\\", "/").strip("/"): file_path for file_path in production_files
+    }
+    for candidate in candidates:
+        normalized_candidate = candidate.replace("\\", "/").strip("/")
+        if normalized_candidate in normalized_production:
+            return normalized_production[normalized_candidate]
+        suffix = f"/{normalized_candidate}"
+        for normalized_path, original in normalized_production.items():
+            if normalized_path.endswith(suffix):
+                return original
     return None
```

diff --git a/desloppify/languages/_framework/treesitter/_imports.py b/desloppify/languages/_framework/treesitter/_imports.py
index 9025cf4..91aaa55 100644
--- a/desloppify/languages/_framework/treesitter/_imports.py
+++ b/desloppify/languages/_framework/treesitter/_imports.py
@@ -357,7 +357,33 @@ def resolve_lua_import(import_text: str, source_file: str, scan_path: str) -> st
 
 
 def resolve_swift_import(import_text: str, source_file: str, scan_path: str) -> str | None:
-    """Swift uses module-level imports — not resolvable to individual files."""
+    """Best-effort local Swift import resolution.
+
+    Swift imports are usually module-level and often external. Resolve only when
+    the import path clearly matches a local ``.swift`` file.
+    """
+    text = import_text.strip()
+    if not text:
+        return None
+
+    module_path = text.replace(".", os.sep)
+    leaf = module_path.split(os.sep)[-1]
+    source_dir = os.path.dirname(source_file)
+
+    candidates = [
+        os.path.join(source_dir, module_path + ".swift"),
+        os.path.join(scan_path, module_path + ".swift"),
+        os.path.join(scan_path, "Sources", module_path + ".swift"),
+        os.path.join(scan_path, "Sources", module_path, f"{leaf}.swift"),
+        os.path.join(scan_path, module_path, f"{leaf}.swift"),
+    ]
+    seen: set[str] = set()
+    for candidate in candidates:
+        if candidate in seen:
+            continue
+        seen.add(candidate)
+        if os.path.isfile(candidate):
+            return candidate
     return None
```

+        """Dict assigned into another dict key should be treated as escaped."""
+        path = _write_py(
+            tmp_path,
+            """\
+            def stage():
+                meta = {}
+                child = {}
+                child["a"] = 1
+                child["b"] = 2
+                child["c"] = 3
+                meta["child"] = child
+                return meta
+        """,
+        )
+        entries, _ = detect_dict_key_flow(path)
+        assert "dead_write" not in _kinds(entries)
+
+    def test_no_dead_write_when_nested_in_list_return(self, tmp_path):
+        """Dict returned inside nested list/tuple structures should be escaped."""
+        path = _write_py(
+            tmp_path,
+            """\
+            def combined():
+                item = {}
+                item["a"] = 1
+                item["b"] = 2
+                item["c"] = 3
+                return [("label", item)]
+        """,
+        )
+        entries, _ = detect_dict_key_flow(path)
+        assert "dead_write" not in _kinds(entries)
+

---

Here is the concrete assessment:

## Test File Analysis

### Review Test Files (5 files): CLEAN MOVE, test counts preserved

The pattern used across all 5 review test files is identical:
- Tests were moved from `test_*.py` into `*_cases.py` files (new untracked files)
- The original `test_*.py` files became thin wrappers that import from cases and re-export via `globals()`
- The `__module__` attribute is patched so pytest shows the correct module name

**Test count verification:**

| File | Original | Cases | Match? |
|------|----------|-------|--------|
| `test_review_commands` | 69 | 69 | Exact |
| `test_review_coverage` | 53 | 53 | Exact |
| `test_review_misc` | 61 | 61 | Exact |
| `test_review_submodules` | 72 | 72 | Exact |
| `test_work_queue` | 29 | 30 | **NOT exact** |

### `test_work_queue.py` / `work_queue_cases.py`: NOT a pure move -- behavioral changes

**6 tests were DELETED** (not present anywhere in the codebase):
- `test_tier_fallback_selects_nearest_non_empty_tier`
- `test_no_tier_fallback_returns_empty_with_reason`
- `test_review_items_ranked_by_tier_like_mechanical`
- `test_subjective_interleave_guardrail_applies_with_default_count_limit`
- `test_tier` (partial match)
- `test_available_tiers_reflects_populated_tiers`

**7 tests were ADDED** (new, not present in original):
- `test_collapse_clusters_preserves_order`
- `test_high_impact_subjective_before_low_impact_mechanical`
- `test_impact_fallback_when_no_dimension_scores`
- `test_impact_sort_with_count_limit`
- `test_queue_contains_mechanical_and_synthetic_subjective_items`
- `test_review_items_ranked_alongside_mechanical`
- `test_subjective_items_always_appear_alongside_mechanical`

**Several existing tests were weakened** -- assertions about ordering and tier values were replaced with simpler set-membership checks. For example, `test_review_finding_uses_natural_tier` originally asserted `effective_tier == 2` and `effective_tier == 3`; now it just checks the IDs exist in a dict.

**The underlying model changed**: ranking went from "tier asc" to "estimated_impact desc". The `QueueBuildOptions` lost `tier` and `no_tier_fallback` fields. The `empty_state` test dropped assertions about `tier_counts`, `available_tiers`, `requested_tier`, `selected_tier`, and `fallback_reason`.

This is a **deliberate removal of the tier-based queue system** in favor of an impact-based system.

### `test_epic_synthesis.py` (DELETED) -> `test_epic_triage.py` (NEW): Rename + rewrite

- The file was deleted (732 lines) and replaced with a new file (752 lines, 58 tests vs original 57)
- Class names renamed: `TestApplySynthesisToPlan` -> `TestApplyTriageToPlan`, etc.
- 6 old tests deleted, 7 new tests added -- not a pure rename
- The underlying `epic_synthesis.py` module was renamed to `epic_triage.py` (same pattern for CLI handlers and playbook)

### `test_cmd_next.py`: Tier system removal

191 lines removed. Three entire test methods deleted:
- `test_tier_navigator_always_printed`
- `test_tier_fallback_message_and_payload`
- `test_no_tier_fallback_strict_empty_guidance`

Additionally, all remaining test mock payloads had `tier`, `effective_tier`, `tier_counts`, `requested_tier`, `selected_tier`, `fallback_reason`, and `available_tiers` keys stripped out. The `_args()` helper lost `tier` and `no_tier_fallback` arguments. The `check_tool_staleness` monkeypatch was removed.

### `test_queue_progress.py`: Pure additions

16 new test functions added, 0 removed. Tests added for new `QueueBreakdown`, `format_queue_headline`, `format_queue_block`, and `plan_aware_queue_breakdown` functions.

---

## Language Plugin Analysis

### Python `deps.py`, `deps_resolution.py`, `deps_dynamic.py`: CLEAN extraction

**`deps.py`** (216 lines removed): All private functions (`_resolve_python_from_import`, `_resolve_python_import`, `_resolve_relative_import`, `_resolve_absolute_import`, `_try_resolve_path`, `find_python_dynamic_imports`) were extracted into two new files:
- **`deps_resolution.py`** (149 lines): Import resolution logic. Functions renamed from private (`_`) to public. The import `PROJECT_ROOT` changed source from `desloppify.core._internal.text_utils` to `desloppify.core.text_api`. Logic is byte-for-byte equivalent.
- **`deps_dynamic.py`** (52 lines): `find_python_dynamic_imports()` -- exact logic, minor style changes (inverted if-condition).

The parent `deps.py` re-imports and re-exports via `__all__`. The `LOGGER` duplicate was cleaned up (was both `LOGGER` and `logger`; now just `logger`).

### Python `smells.py` / `smells_runtime.py`: CLEAN extraction

**`smells.py`** (307 lines removed): All runtime functions (`_build_string_line_set`, `_match_is_in_string`, `_walk_except_blocks`, `_is_broad_except`, `_detect_empty_except`, `_detect_swallowed_errors`, and the body of `detect_smells`) were extracted to **`smells_runtime.py`** (272 lines).

The `detect_smells` **public signature** is preserved (`path: Path -> tuple[list[dict], int]`). Internally it delegates to the runtime version with injected parameters (`smell_checks`, `is_test_path_fn`, `logger`). The `SMELL_CHECKS` list stays in `smells.py` (configuration) while runtime scanning moves to `smells_runtime.py`.

Minor style changes: some comment headers removed ("# Regex-based detectors", "# Multi-line detectors", "# AST-based detectors"), reformatted `_smell()` calls. No logic changes.

### Python `phases.py` / `phases_runtime.py`: CLEAN extraction

**`phases.py`** (188 lines removed): `phase_structural()` and `phase_coupling()` bodies extracted to `phases_runtime.py` as `run_phase_structural()` and `run_phase_coupling()`. The wrapper functions in `phases.py` delegate with explicit keyword args. All imports that were only needed by the extracted code moved to the runtime file.

### TypeScript `deps.py` / `deps_resolve.py`: CLEAN extraction

**`deps.py`** (184 lines removed): `_load_tsconfig_paths`, `_parse_tsconfig_paths`, `_extract_paths`, `_iter_resolve_candidates`, `_resolve_alias`, and `_resolve_module` extracted to **`deps_resolve.py`** (166 lines). Functions renamed from private to public. The `lru_cache` for tsconfig loading was preserved. Logic equivalent.

### TypeScript `_smell_helpers.py`: CLEAN extraction

**33 lines removed**: The `scan_code()` generator was moved to `desloppify/languages/typescript/syntax/scanner.py` and is now imported from there. No logic change.

### C# `deps.py` / `deps_support.py`: CLEAN extraction

**370 lines removed**: All the C# dependency graph infrastructure (regex patterns, csproj parsing, namespace resolution, graph building, CLI formatters) extracted to **`deps_support.py`** (377 lines). The parent file shrunk from ~300+ lines of implementation to ~37 lines of imports and thin wrappers.

### Dart `extractors.py`: Code sharing improvement

`_find_matching_brace` replaced with a call to `desloppify.languages.csharp._parse_helpers.find_matching_brace`. The logic was identical between the two languages; this eliminates duplication.

### All language `commands.py` files (Go, Dart, GDScript, C#): Pattern change

All four switched from defining `get_detect_commands()` as a function to using a new factory: `make_standard_detect_registry_getter(...)`. This is a pure mechanical refactor -- the new factory returns a callable with the same signature.

### Framework `discovery.py`: Re-entrancy fix

The `registry_state.set_load_attempted(True)` call was moved **earlier** in `load_all()` -- before plugin imports instead of after. This prevents infinite recursion if a plugin's import transitively calls `load_all()` again (via `get_lang()`).

### Go `test_coverage.py`: NEW LOGIC

`resolve_import_spec` went from a stub that always returned `None` ("Go tests use same-package layout, not import spec resolution") to a real implementation with candidate-based resolution (~37 new lines of logic). This is an actual feature addition, not a refactor.

### Swift `resolve_swift_import` in `treesitter/_imports.py`: NEW LOGIC

Similarly went from a no-op stub to a real best-effort resolver that checks 5 candidate file paths. Another feature addition.

### Python `dict_keys_visitor.py` + tests: BUG FIX

Added `_mark_assignment_escape()` to track when a dict is assigned into a container slot (e.g. `meta["child"] = child`), and extended `_mark_returned_or_passed` to handle `List`, `Set`, and nested `Dict` returns. Two new tests confirm the fix prevents false positive `dead_write` reports for dicts nested in containers.

---

## Summary

**Tests:**
- 4 of 5 review test file moves are **exact** (zero test loss).
- `test_work_queue` has 6 tests deleted and 7 added -- this reflects a deliberate **tier system removal** in favor of impact-based ranking.
- `test_epic_synthesis` -> `test_epic_triage` is a **rename + rewrite**, not a pure move.
- `test_cmd_next` had 3 tests deleted and many assertions stripped -- also part of the tier removal.
- `test_queue_progress` gained 16 net new tests for new `QueueBreakdown` functionality.

**Language plugins:**
- All extractions (Python deps/smells/phases, TS deps/smells, C# deps) are **clean** -- logic preserved, public APIs maintained, only internal organization changed.
- The `commands.py` pattern change across 4 languages is mechanical.
- The framework `discovery.py` change is a targeted re-entrancy bug fix.

**Hidden in the refactoring:**
- Go `resolve_import_spec` and Swift `resolve_swift_import` are **new feature implementations**, not refactors.
- Python `dict_keys_visitor.py` has a **bug fix** (false positive prevention for nested container assignments).
- The entire **tier-based queue system is being removed** in favor of impact-based ranking -- this is a significant behavioral change disguised within the extraction/refactor diff.

   128→        if not isinstance(files, list):
   129→            continue
   130→        for raw in files:
   131→            if not isinstance(raw, str):
   132→                continue
   133→            path = raw.strip().strip(",'\"")
   134→            if not path or path in {".", ".."}:
   135→                continue
   136→            if path.endswith("/"):
   137→                continue
   138→            if path in seen:
   139→                continue
   140→            seen.add(path)
   141→            reviewed.append(path)
   142→    return reviewed
   143→
   144→
   145→def _collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   146→    *,
   147→    batches: list[dict[str, object]],
   148→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   149→) -> list[str]:
   150→    """Compatibility wrapper for reviewed-file collection."""
   151→    return _collect_reviewed_files_from_batches_impl(
   152→        batches=batches,
   153→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   154→    )
   155→
   156→
   157→from .runtime.policy import resolve_batch_run_policy
   158→
   159→
   160→def _normalize_dimension_list_impl(raw: object) -> list[str]:
   161→    """Normalize dimension collections to a stable, de-duplicated list."""
   162→    if not isinstance(raw, list):
   163→        return []
   164→    out: list[str] = []
   165→    seen: set[str] = set()
   166→    for item in raw:
   167→        if not isinstance(item, str):
   168→            continue
   169→        dim = item.strip()
   170→        if not dim or dim in seen:
   171→            continue
   172→        seen.add(dim)
   173→        out.append(dim)
   174→    return out
   175→
   176→
   177→def _normalize_dimension_list(raw: object) -> list[str]:
   178→    """Compatibility wrapper for dimension list normalization."""
   179→    return _normalize_dimension_list_impl(raw)
   180→
   181→
   182→def _scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name: str) -> list[str]:
   183→    """Return default scored subjective dimensions for one language."""
   184→    try:
   185→        default_dims, _, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   186→    except (ValueError, RuntimeError):
   187→        return []
   188→    return _normalize_dimension_list(default_dims)
   189→
   190→
   191→def _missing_scored_dimensions(
   192→    *,
   193→    selected_dims: list[str],
   194→    scored_dims: list[str],
   195→) -> list[str]:
   196→    selected = set(selected_dims)
   197→    return [dim for dim in scored_dims if dim not in selected]
   198→
   199→
   200→def _missing_dimensions_command(*, missing_dims: list[str], scan_path: str) -> str:
   201→    """Return rerun command for missing subjective dimensions."""
   202→    base = "desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import"
   203→    if scan_path and scan_path != ".":
   204→        base += f" --path {shlex.quote(scan_path)}"
   205→    if missing_dims:
   206→        base += f" --dimensions {','.join(missing_dims)}"
   207→    return base
   208→
   209→
   210→def _print_preflight_dimension_scope_notice_impl(
   211→    *,
   212→    selected_dims: list[str],
   213→    scored_dims: list[str],
   214→    explicit_selection: bool,
   215→    scan_path: str,
   216→    colorize_fn,
   217→) -> None:
   218→    """Print trigger-time notice when run scope is a scored-dimension subset."""
   219→    if not scored_dims:
   220→        return
   221→    missing_dims = _missing_scored_dimensions(
   222→        selected_dims=selected_dims,
   223→        scored_dims=scored_dims,
   224→    )
   225→    if not missing_dims:
   226→        return
   227→
   228→    covered_count = len([dim for dim in selected_dims if dim in set(scored_dims)])
   229→    scope_reason = (
   230→        "explicit --dimensions selection"
   231→        if explicit_selection
   232→        else "language default review dimension set"
   233→    )
   234→    tone = "yellow" if explicit_selection else "red"
   235→    print(
   236→        colorize_fn(
   237→            "  WARNING: this run targets "
   238→            f"{covered_count}/{len(scored_dims)} scored subjective dimensions "
   239→            f"({scope_reason}).",
   240→            tone,
   241→        )
   242→    )
   243→    preview = ", ".join(missing_dims[:5])
   244→    if len(missing_dims) > 5:
   245→        preview = f"{preview}, +{len(missing_dims) - 5} more"
   246→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Missing from this run: {preview}", "yellow"))
   247→    print(
   248→        colorize_fn(
   249→            "  Rerun missing dimensions: "
   250→            f"`{_missing_dimensions_command(missing_dims=missing_dims, scan_path=scan_path)}`",
   251→            "dim",
   252→        )
   253→    )
   254→
   255→
   256→def _print_preflight_dimension_scope_notice(
   257→    *,
   258→    selected_dims: list[str],
   259→    scored_dims: list[str],
   260→    explicit_selection: bool,
   261→    scan_path: str,
   262→    colorize_fn,
   263→) -> None:
   264→    """Compatibility wrapper for preflight scope messaging."""
   265→    _print_preflight_dimension_scope_notice_impl(
   266→        selected_dims=selected_dims,
   267→        scored_dims=scored_dims,
   268→        explicit_selection=explicit_selection,
   269→        scan_path=scan_path,
   270→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   271→    )
   272→
   273→
   274→def _print_import_dimension_coverage_notice_impl(
   275→    *,
   276→    assessed_dims: list[str],
   277→    scored_dims: list[str],
   278→    scan_path: str,
   279→    colorize_fn,
   280→) -> list[str]:
   281→    """Print result-time notice when merged import covers only a subset."""
   282→    if not scored_dims:
   283→        return []
   284→    missing_dims = _missing_scored_dimensions(
   285→        selected_dims=assessed_dims,
   286→        scored_dims=scored_dims,
   287→    )
   288→    if not missing_dims:
   289→        return []
   290→
   291→    covered_count = len([dim for dim in assessed_dims if dim in set(scored_dims)])
   292→    print(
   293→        colorize_fn(
   294→            "  Coverage gap: imported assessments for "
   295→            f"{covered_count}/{len(scored_dims)} scored subjective dimensions.",
   296→            "yellow",
   297→        )
   298→    )
   299→    preview = ", ".join(missing_dims[:5])
   300→    if len(missing_dims) > 5:
   301→        preview = f"{preview}, +{len(missing_dims) - 5} more"
   302→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Still missing: {preview}", "yellow"))
   303→    print(
   304→        colorize_fn(
   305→            "  Run to cover missing dimensions: "
   306→            f"`{_missing_dimensions_command(missing_dims=missing_dims, scan_path=scan_path)}`",
   307→            "dim",
   308→        )
   309→    )
   310→    return missing_dims
   311→
   312→
   313→def _print_import_dimension_coverage_notice(
   314→    *,
   315→    assessed_dims: list[str],
   316→    scored_dims: list[str],
   317→    scan_path: str,
   318→    colorize_fn,
   319→) -> list[str]:
   320→    """Compatibility wrapper for import coverage messaging."""
   321→    return _print_import_dimension_coverage_notice_impl(
   322→        assessed_dims=assessed_dims,
   323→        scored_dims=scored_dims,
   324→        scan_path=scan_path,
   325→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   326→    )
