```
   328→
   329→def _append_run_log_line(run_log_path: Path, message: str) -> None:
   330→    """Append one timestamped line to the run log (best effort)."""
   331→    line = f"{datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec='seconds')} {message}\n"
   332→    try:
   333→        with run_log_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   334→            handle.write(line)
   335→    except OSError:
   336→        return
   337→
   338→
   339→def _run_batch_prompt(
   340→    *,
   341→    prompt: str,
   342→    output_file: Path,
   343→    log_file: Path,
   344→    project_root: Path,
   345→    run_codex_batch_fn,
   346→) -> int:
   347→    """Execute one batch prompt via the injected runner function."""
   348→    return run_codex_batch_fn(
   349→        prompt=prompt,
   350→        repo_root=project_root,
   351→        output_file=output_file,
   352→        log_file=log_file,
   353→    )
   354→
   355→
   356→def _run_batch_task(
   357→    *,
   358→    batch_index: int,
   359→    prompt_path: Path,
   360→    output_path: Path,
   361→    log_path: Path,
   362→    project_root: Path,
   363→    run_codex_batch_fn,
   364→) -> int:
   365→    """Read one prompt artifact and execute its runner task."""
   366→    try:
   367→        prompt = prompt_path.read_text()
   368→    except OSError as exc:
   369→        raise RuntimeError(
   370→            f"unable to read prompt for batch #{batch_index + 1}: {prompt_path}"
   371→        ) from exc
   372→    return _run_batch_prompt(
   373→        prompt=prompt,
   374→        output_file=output_path,
   375→        log_file=log_path,
   376→        project_root=project_root,
   377→        run_codex_batch_fn=run_codex_batch_fn,
   378→    )
   379→
   380→
   381→def _build_batch_tasks(
   382→    *,
   383→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   384→    prompt_files: dict[int, Path],
   385→    output_files: dict[int, Path],
   386→    log_files: dict[int, Path],
   387→    project_root: Path,
   388→    run_codex_batch_fn,
   389→) -> dict[int, object]:
   390→    """Build index→callable task map for execute_batches."""
   391→    tasks: dict[int, object] = {}
   392→    for batch_index in selected_indexes:
   393→        tasks[batch_index] = partial(
   394→            _run_batch_task,
   395→            batch_index=batch_index,
   396→            prompt_path=prompt_files[batch_index],
   397→            output_path=output_files[batch_index],
   398→            log_path=log_files[batch_index],
   399→            project_root=project_root,
   400→            run_codex_batch_fn=run_codex_batch_fn,
   401→        )
   402→    return tasks
   403→
   404→
   405→def _record_execution_issue(append_run_log_fn, batch_index: int, exc: Exception) -> None:
   406→    """Record one execute_batches callback/task failure in run.log."""
   407→    if batch_index < 0:
   408→        append_run_log_fn(f"execution-error heartbeat error={exc}")
   409→        return
   410→    append_run_log_fn(f"execution-error batch={batch_index + 1} error={exc}")
   411→
   412→
   413→def _write_run_summary(
   414→    *,
   415→    run_summary_path: Path,
   416→    summary_created_at: str,
   417→    stamp: str,
   418→    runner: str,
   419→    run_parallel: bool,
   420→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   421→    successful_batches: list[int],
   422→    failed_batches: list[int],
   423→    allow_partial: bool,
   424→    max_parallel_batches: int,
   425→    batch_timeout_seconds: int,
   426→    batch_max_retries: int,
   427→    batch_retry_backoff_seconds: float,
   428→    heartbeat_seconds: float,
   429→    stall_warning_seconds: int,
   430→    stall_kill_seconds: int,
   431→    immutable_packet_path: Path,
   432→    prompt_packet_path: Path,
   433→    run_dir: Path,
   434→    logs_dir: Path,
   435→    run_log_path: Path,
   436→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   437→    safe_write_text_fn,
   438→    colorize_fn,
   439→    append_run_log_fn,
   440→    interrupted: bool = False,
   441→    interruption_reason: str | None = None,
   442→) -> None:
   443→    """Write run_summary.json and emit a trace line to run.log."""
   444→    run_summary: dict[str, object] = {
   445→        "created_at": summary_created_at,
   446→        "run_stamp": stamp,
   447→        "runner": runner,
   448→        "parallel": run_parallel,
   449→        "selected_batches": [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes],
   450→        "successful_batches": successful_batches,
   451→        "failed_batches": failed_batches,
   452→        "allow_partial": allow_partial,
   453→        "max_parallel_batches": max_parallel_batches if run_parallel else 1,
   454→        "batch_timeout_seconds": batch_timeout_seconds,
   455→        "batch_max_retries": batch_max_retries,
   456→        "batch_retry_backoff_seconds": batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   457→        "batch_heartbeat_seconds": heartbeat_seconds if run_parallel else None,
   458→        "batch_stall_warning_seconds": stall_warning_seconds if run_parallel else None,
   459→        "batch_stall_kill_seconds": stall_kill_seconds,
   460→        "immutable_packet": str(immutable_packet_path),
   461→        "blind_packet": str(prompt_packet_path),
   462→        "run_dir": str(run_dir),
   463→        "logs_dir": str(logs_dir),
   464→        "run_log": str(run_log_path),
   465→        "batches": batch_status,
   466→    }
   467→    if interrupted:
   468→        run_summary["interrupted"] = True
   469→        if interruption_reason:
   470→            run_summary["interruption_reason"] = interruption_reason
   471→    safe_write_text_fn(run_summary_path, json.dumps(run_summary, indent=2) + "\n")
   472→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Run summary: {run_summary_path}", "dim"))
   473→    append_run_log_fn(f"run-summary {run_summary_path}")
   474→
   475→
   476→def do_run_batches(
   477→    args,
   478→    state,
   479→    lang,
   480→    state_file,
   481→    *,
   482→    config: dict | None,
   483→    run_stamp_fn,
   484→    load_or_prepare_packet_fn,
   485→    selected_batch_indexes_fn,
   486→    prepare_run_artifacts_fn,
   487→    run_codex_batch_fn,
   488→    execute_batches_fn,
   489→    collect_batch_results_fn,
   490→    print_failures_fn,
   491→    print_failures_and_exit_fn,
   492→    merge_batch_results_fn,
   493→    build_import_provenance_fn,
   494→    do_import_fn,
   495→    run_followup_scan_fn,
   496→    safe_write_text_fn,
   497→    colorize_fn,
   498→    project_root: Path,
   499→    subagent_runs_dir: Path,
   500→) -> None:
   501→    """Run holistic investigation batches with a local subagent runner."""
   502→    config = config or {}
   503→    runner = getattr(args, "runner", "codex")
   504→    _validate_runner(runner, colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   505→    allow_partial = bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False))
   506→    policy = resolve_batch_run_policy(args)
   507→    run_parallel = policy.run_parallel
   508→    max_parallel_batches = policy.max_parallel_batches
   509→    heartbeat_seconds = policy.heartbeat_seconds
   510→    batch_timeout_seconds = policy.batch_timeout_seconds
   511→    batch_max_retries = policy.batch_max_retries
   512→    batch_retry_backoff_seconds = policy.batch_retry_backoff_seconds
   513→    stall_warning_seconds = policy.stall_warning_seconds
   514→    stall_kill_seconds = policy.stall_kill_seconds
   515→
   516→    stamp = run_stamp_fn()
   517→    packet, immutable_packet_path, prompt_packet_path = load_or_prepare_packet_fn(
   518→        args,
   519→        state=state,
   520→        lang=lang,
   521→        config=config,
   522→        stamp=stamp,
   523→    )
   524→
   525→    scan_path = str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or ".")
   526→    packet_dimensions = _normalize_dimension_list(packet.get("dimensions", []))
   527→    scored_dimensions = _scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang.name)
   528→    _print_preflight_dimension_scope_notice(
   529→        selected_dims=packet_dimensions,
   530→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   531→        explicit_selection=bool(getattr(args, "dimensions", None)),
   532→        scan_path=scan_path,
   533→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   534→    )
   535→    suggested_prepare_cmd = f"desloppify review --prepare --path {scan_path}"
   536→    batches = _require_batches(
   537→        packet,
   538→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   539→        suggested_prepare_cmd=suggested_prepare_cmd,
   540→    )
   541→
   542→    selected_indexes = selected_batch_indexes_fn(args, batch_count=len(batches))
   543→    total_batches = len(selected_indexes)
   544→    effective_workers = min(total_batches, max_parallel_batches) if run_parallel else 1
   545→    waves = max(1, math.ceil(total_batches / max(1, effective_workers)))
   546→    worst_case_seconds = waves * batch_timeout_seconds
   547→    worst_case_minutes = max(1, math.ceil(worst_case_seconds / 60))
   548→    print(
   549→        colorize_fn(
   550→            "  Runtime expectation: "
   551→            f"{total_batches} batch(es), workers={effective_workers}, "
   552→            f"timeout-per-batch={int(batch_timeout_seconds / 60)}m, "
   553→            f"worst-case upper bound ~{worst_case_minutes}m.",
   554→            "dim",
   555→        )
   556→    )
   557→    run_dir, logs_dir, prompt_files, output_files, log_files = prepare_run_artifacts_fn(
   558→        stamp=stamp,
   559→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   560→        batches=batches,
   561→        packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   562→        run_root=subagent_runs_dir,
   563→        repo_root=project_root,
   564→    )
   565→    raw_run_log_file = getattr(args, "run_log_file", None)
   566→    run_log_file_value = (
   567→        raw_run_log_file.strip()
   568→        if isinstance(raw_run_log_file, str) and raw_run_log_file.strip()
   569→        else None
   570→    )
   571→    if run_log_file_value is not None:
   572→        candidate = Path(run_log_file_value).expanduser()
   573→        run_log_path = candidate if candidate.is_absolute() else project_root / candidate
   574→    else:
   575→        run_log_path = run_dir / "run.log"
   576→    run_log_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   577→    append_run_log = partial(_append_run_log_line, run_log_path)
   578→
   579→    append_run_log(
   580→        "run-start "
   581→        f"runner={runner} parallel={run_parallel} max_parallel={max_parallel_batches} "
   582→        f"timeout={batch_timeout_seconds}s heartbeat={heartbeat_seconds:.1f}s "
   583→        f"stall_warning={stall_warning_seconds}s stall_kill={stall_kill_seconds}s "
   584→        f"retries={batch_max_retries} "
   585→        f"retry_backoff={batch_retry_backoff_seconds:.1f}s upper_bound={worst_case_minutes}m "
   586→        f"selected={[idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes]}"
   587→    )
   588→    append_run_log(f"run-path {run_dir}")
   589→    append_run_log(f"packet {immutable_packet_path}")
   590→    append_run_log(f"blind-packet {prompt_packet_path}")
   591→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Live run log: {run_log_path}", "dim"))
   592→
   593→    if getattr(args, "dry_run", False):
   594→        print(
   595→            colorize_fn(
   596→                "  Dry run only: prompts generated, runner execution skipped.", "yellow"
   597→            )
   598→        )
   599→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Immutable packet: {immutable_packet_path}", "dim"))
   600→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Blind packet: {prompt_packet_path}", "dim"))
   601→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Prompts: {run_dir / 'prompts'}", "dim"))
   602→        append_run_log("run-finished dry-run")
   603→        return
   604→    tasks = _build_batch_tasks(
   605→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   606→        prompt_files=prompt_files,
   607→        output_files=output_files,
   608→        log_files=log_files,
   609→        project_root=project_root,
   610→        run_codex_batch_fn=run_codex_batch_fn,
   611→    )
   612→
   613→    batch_positions = {batch_idx: pos + 1 for pos, batch_idx in enumerate(selected_indexes)}
   614→    summary_created_at = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   615→    stall_warned_batches: set[int] = set()
   616→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {
   617→        str(idx + 1): {
   618→            "position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0),
   619→            "status": "pending",
   620→            "prompt_path": str(prompt_files[idx]),
   621→            "result_path": str(output_files[idx]),
   622→            "log_path": str(log_files[idx]),
   623→        }
   624→        for idx in selected_indexes
   625→    }
   626→
   627→    if run_parallel:
   628→        print(
   629→            colorize_fn(
   630→                "  Parallel runner config: "
   631→                f"max-workers={min(total_batches, max_parallel_batches)}, "
   632→                f"heartbeat={heartbeat_seconds:.1f}s",
   633→                "dim",
   634→            )
   635→        )
   636→
   637→    def _report_progress(
   638→        batch_index: int,
   639→        event: str,
   640→        code: int | None = None,
   641→        **details,
   642→    ) -> None:
   643→        if event == "heartbeat":
   644→            active = details.get("active_batches")
   645→            queued = details.get("queued_batches", [])
   646→            elapsed = details.get("elapsed_seconds", {})
   647→            if not isinstance(active, list):
   648→                active = []
   649→            if not isinstance(queued, list):
   650→                queued = []
   651→            if not active and not queued:
   652→                return
   653→            segments: list[str] = []
   654→            for idx in active[:6]:
   655→                secs = 0
   656→                if isinstance(elapsed, dict):
   657→                    raw_secs = elapsed.get(idx, 0)
   658→                    secs = int(raw_secs) if isinstance(raw_secs, int | float) else 0
   659→                segments.append(f"#{idx + 1}:{secs}s")
   660→            if len(active) > 6:
   661→                segments.append(f"+{len(active) - 6} more")
   662→            queued_segment = ""
   663→            if queued:
   664→                queued_segment = f", queued {len(queued)}"
   665→            print(
   666→                colorize_fn(
   667→                    "  Batch heartbeat: "
   668→                    f"{len(active)}/{total_batches} active{queued_segment} "
   669→                    f"({', '.join(segments) if segments else 'running batches pending'})",
   670→                    "dim",
   671→                )
   672→            )
   673→            append_run_log(
   674→                "heartbeat "
   675→                f"active={[idx + 1 for idx in active]} queued={[idx + 1 for idx in queued]} "
   676→                f"elapsed={{{', '.join(f'{idx + 1}:{elapsed.get(idx, 0)}' for idx in active)}}}"
   677→            )
   678→            if stall_warning_seconds > 0 and isinstance(elapsed, dict):
   679→                slow_active = [
   680→                    idx
   681→                    for idx in active
   682→                    if isinstance(elapsed.get(idx), int | float)
   683→                    and int(elapsed.get(idx) or 0) >= stall_warning_seconds
   684→                ]
   685→                newly_warned = [idx for idx in slow_active if idx not in stall_warned_batches]
   686→                if newly_warned:
   687→                    stall_warned_batches.update(newly_warned)
   688→                    warning_message = (
   689→                        "  Stall warning: batches "
   690→                        f"{[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]} exceeded "
   691→                        f"{stall_warning_seconds}s elapsed. "
   692→                        "This may be normal for long runs; review run.log and batch logs."
   693→                    )
   694→                    print(colorize_fn(warning_message, "yellow"))
   695→                    append_run_log(
   696→                        "stall-warning "
   697→                        f"threshold={stall_warning_seconds}s batches={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]}"
   698→                    )
   699→            return
   700→
   701→        position = batch_positions.get(batch_index, 0)
   702→        key = str(batch_index + 1)
   703→        state = batch_status.setdefault(
   704→            key,
   705→            {
   706→                "position": position,
   707→                "status": "pending",
   708→                "prompt_path": str(prompt_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   709→                "result_path": str(output_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   710→                "log_path": str(log_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   711→            },
   712→        )
   713→        if event == "queued":
   714→            state["status"] = "queued"
   715→            print(
   716→                colorize_fn(
   717→                    f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} queued (#{batch_index + 1})",
   718→                    "dim",
   719→                )
   720→            )
   721→            append_run_log(f"batch-queued batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches}")
   722→            return
   723→        if event == "start":
   724→            state["status"] = "running"
   725→            state["started_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   726→            print(
   727→                colorize_fn(
   728→                    f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} started (#{batch_index + 1})",
   729→                    "dim",
   730→                )
   731→            )
   732→            append_run_log(f"batch-start batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches}")
   733→            return
   734→        if event == "done":
   735→            status = "done" if code == 0 else f"failed ({code})"
   736→            tone = "dim" if code == 0 else "yellow"
   737→            elapsed_seconds = details.get("elapsed_seconds")
   738→            elapsed_suffix = ""
   739→            if isinstance(elapsed_seconds, int | float):
   740→                elapsed_suffix = f" in {int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))}s"
   741→                state["elapsed_seconds"] = int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))
   742→            state["status"] = "succeeded" if code == 0 else "failed"
   743→            state["exit_code"] = int(code) if isinstance(code, int) else code
   744→            state["completed_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   745→            if batch_index in stall_warned_batches:
   746→                stall_warned_batches.discard(batch_index)
   747→            print(
   748→                colorize_fn(
   749→                    f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} {status}{elapsed_suffix} (#{batch_index + 1})",
   750→                    tone,
   751→                )
   752→            )
   753→            append_run_log(
   754→                f"batch-done batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches} "
   755→                f"code={code} elapsed={state.get('elapsed_seconds', 0)}"
   756→            )
   757→
   758→    record_execution_issue = partial(_record_execution_issue, append_run_log)
   759→    run_summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   760→    write_run_summary = partial(
   761→        _write_run_summary,
   762→        run_summary_path=run_summary_path,
   763→        summary_created_at=summary_created_at,
   764→        stamp=stamp,
   765→        runner=runner,
   766→        run_parallel=run_parallel,
   767→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   768→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   769→        max_parallel_batches=max_parallel_batches,
   770→        batch_timeout_seconds=batch_timeout_seconds,
   771→        batch_max_retries=batch_max_retries,
   772→        batch_retry_backoff_seconds=batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   773→        heartbeat_seconds=heartbeat_seconds,
   774→        stall_warning_seconds=stall_warning_seconds,
   775→        stall_kill_seconds=stall_kill_seconds,
   776→        immutable_packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   777→        prompt_packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   778→        run_dir=run_dir,
   779→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
   780→        run_log_path=run_log_path,
   781→        batch_status=batch_status,
   782→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text_fn,
   783→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   784→        append_run_log_fn=append_run_log,
   785→    )
   786→
   787→    try:
   788→        execution_failures = execute_batches_fn(
   789→            tasks=tasks,
   790→            run_parallel=run_parallel,
   791→            progress_fn=_report_progress,
   792→            error_log_fn=record_execution_issue,
   793→            max_parallel_workers=max_parallel_batches,
   794→            heartbeat_seconds=heartbeat_seconds,
   795→        )
   796→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   797→        for idx in selected_indexes:
   798→            key = str(idx + 1)
   799→            state = batch_status.setdefault(
   800→                key,
   801→                {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   802→            )
   803→            if state.get("status") in {"pending", "queued", "running"}:
   804→                state["status"] = "interrupted"
   805→        write_run_summary(
   806→            successful_batches=[],
   807→            failed_batches=[],
   808→            interrupted=True,
   809→            interruption_reason="keyboard_interrupt",
   810→        )
   811→        append_run_log("run-interrupted reason=keyboard_interrupt")
   812→        raise SystemExit(130) from None
   813→
   814→    allowed_dims = {
   815→        str(dim) for dim in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(dim, str)
   816→    }
   817→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results_fn(
   818→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   819→        failures=execution_failures,
   820→        output_files=output_files,
   821→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   822→    )
   823→
   824→    execution_failure_set = set(execution_failures)
   825→    failure_set = set(failures)
   826→    successful_indexes = sorted(idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in failure_set)
   827→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   828→        key = str(idx + 1)
   829→        state = batch_status.setdefault(
   830→            key,
   831→            {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   832→        )
   833→        if idx not in failure_set:
   834→            state["status"] = "succeeded"
   835→            continue
   836→        if idx in execution_failure_set:
   837→            state["status"] = "failed"
   838→            continue
   839→        if not output_files[idx].exists():
   840→            state["status"] = "missing_output"
   841→            continue
   842→        state["status"] = "parse_failed"
   843→
   844→    write_run_summary(
   845→        successful_batches=[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes],
   846→        failed_batches=[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)],
   847→    )
   848→
   849→    if failures and (not allow_partial or not batch_results):
   850→        append_run_log(
   851→            f"run-finished failures={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]} mode=exit"
   852→        )
   853→        print_failures_and_exit_fn(
   854→            failures=failures,
   855→            packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   856→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   857→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   858→        )
   859→    elif failures:
   860→        print(
   861→            colorize_fn(
   862→                "  Partial completion enabled: importing successful batches and keeping failed batches open.",
   863→                "yellow",
   864→            )
   865→        )
   866→        print_failures_fn(
   867→            failures=failures,
   868→            packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   869→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   870→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   871→        )
   872→        append_run_log(
   873→            "run-partial "
   874→            f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   875→            f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]}"
   876→        )
   877→
   878→    merged = merge_batch_results_fn(batch_results)
   879→    reviewed_files = _collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   880→        batches=batches,
   881→        selected_indexes=successful_indexes,
   882→    )
   883→    full_sweep_included = any(
   884→        str(batch.get("name", "")).strip().lower() == "full codebase sweep"
   885→        for idx in successful_indexes
   886→        if 0 <= idx < len(batches)
   887→        for batch in [batches[idx]]
   888→        if isinstance(batch, dict)
   889→    )
   890→    review_scope: dict[str, object] = {
   891→        "reviewed_files_count": len(reviewed_files),
   892→        "successful_batch_count": len(successful_indexes),
   893→        "full_sweep_included": full_sweep_included,
   894→    }
   895→    total_files = packet.get("total_files")
   896→    if isinstance(total_files, int) and not isinstance(total_files, bool) and total_files > 0:
   897→        review_scope["total_files"] = total_files
   898→    merged["review_scope"] = review_scope
   899→    if reviewed_files:
   900→        merged["reviewed_files"] = reviewed_files
   901→        print(
   902→            colorize_fn(
   903→                f"  Reviewed files captured for cache refresh: {len(reviewed_files)}",
   904→                "dim",
   905→            )
   906→        )
   907→    merged["provenance"] = build_import_provenance_fn(
   908→        runner=runner,
   909→        blind_packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   910→        run_stamp=stamp,
   911→        batch_indexes=successful_indexes,
   912→    )
   913→    merged_assessment_dims = _normalize_dimension_list(
   914→        list((merged.get("assessments") or {}).keys())
   915→    )
   916→    merged_finding_dims = _normalize_dimension_list(
   917→        [
   918→            finding.get("dimension")
   919→            for finding in (merged.get("findings") or [])
   920→            if isinstance(finding, dict)
   921→        ]
   922→    )
   923→    merged_imported_dims = _normalize_dimension_list(
   924→        merged_assessment_dims + merged_finding_dims
   925→    )
   926→    review_scope["imported_dimensions"] = merged_imported_dims
   927→    missing_after_import = _print_import_dimension_coverage_notice(
   928→        assessed_dims=merged_assessment_dims,
   929→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   930→        scan_path=scan_path,
   931→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   932→    )
   933→    merged["assessment_coverage"] = {
   934→        "scored_dimensions": scored_dimensions,
   935→        "selected_dimensions": packet_dimensions,
   936→        "imported_dimensions": merged_assessment_dims,
   937→        "missing_dimensions": missing_after_import,
   938→    }
   939→    merged_path = run_dir / "holistic_findings_merged.json"
   940→    safe_write_text_fn(merged_path, json.dumps(merged, indent=2) + "\n")
   941→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Merged outputs: {merged_path}", "bold"))
   942→    _print_review_quality(merged.get("review_quality", {}), colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   943→
   944→    try:
   945→        do_import_fn(
   946→            str(merged_path),
   947→            state,
   948→            lang,
   949→            state_file,
   950→            config=config,
   951→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   952→            trusted_assessment_source=True,
   953→            trusted_assessment_label="trusted internal run-batches import",
   954→        )
   955→    except SystemExit as exc:

   957→        raise
   958→    except Exception as exc:

   960→        raise
   961→    append_run_log(

   965→    )
   966→
   967→    if getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False):
```

```
   583→def test_write_packet_snapshot_redacts_target_from_blind_packet(tmp_path):
   584→    packet = {
   585→        "command": "review",
   586→        "config": {"target_strict_score": 98, "noise_budget": 10},
   587→        "narrative": {"headline": "target score pressure"},
   588→        "next_command": "desloppify scan",
   589→        "dimensions": ["high_level_elegance"],
   590→    }
   591→    review_packet_dir = tmp_path / "review_packets"
   592→    blind_path = tmp_path / "review_packet_blind.json"
   593→
   594→    def _safe_write(path: Path, text: str) -> None:
   595→        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   596→        path.write_text(text)
   597→
   598→    packet_path, _ = write_packet_snapshot(
   599→        packet,
   600→        stamp="20260218_160000",
   601→        review_packet_dir=review_packet_dir,
   602→        blind_path=blind_path,
   603→        safe_write_text_fn=_safe_write,
   604→    )
   605→
   606→    immutable_payload = json.loads(packet_path.read_text())
   607→    blind_payload = json.loads(blind_path.read_text())
   608→
   609→    assert immutable_payload["config"]["target_strict_score"] == 98
   610→    assert "target_strict_score" not in blind_payload["config"]
   611→    assert blind_payload["config"]["noise_budget"] == 10
   612→    assert "narrative" not in blind_payload
   613→    assert "next_command" not in blind_payload
   614→
   615→
   616→_P_SETUP = "desloppify.app.commands.review.prepare.review_runtime_mod.setup_lang_concrete"
   617→_P_NARRATIVE = "desloppify.app.commands.review.prepare.narrative_mod.compute_narrative"
   618→_P_REVIEW_PREP = "desloppify.app.commands.review.prepare.review_mod.prepare_holistic_review"
   619→_P_REVIEW_OPTS = "desloppify.app.commands.review.prepare.review_mod.HolisticReviewPrepareOptions"
   620→_P_NARRATIVE_CTX = "desloppify.app.commands.review.prepare.narrative_mod.NarrativeContext"
   621→_P_WRITE_QUERY = "desloppify.app.commands.review.prepare.write_query"
   622→
   623→
   624→def _do_prepare_patched(*, total_files: int = 3, state: dict | None = None, config: dict | None = None):
   625→    """Call do_prepare with mocked dependencies; return captured write_query payload."""
   626→    args = SimpleNamespace(path=".", dimensions=None)
   627→    captured: dict = {}
   628→
   629→    def _fake_write_query(payload):
   630→        captured.update(payload)
   631→
   632→    with (
   633→        patch(_P_SETUP, return_value=(SimpleNamespace(name="python"), [])),
   634→        patch(_P_NARRATIVE, return_value={"headline": "x"}),
   635→        patch(_P_REVIEW_PREP, return_value={
   636→            "total_files": total_files,
   637→            "investigation_batches": [],
   638→            "workflow": [],
   639→        }),
   640→        patch(_P_REVIEW_OPTS, side_effect=lambda **kw: SimpleNamespace(**kw)),
   641→        patch(_P_NARRATIVE_CTX, side_effect=lambda **kw: SimpleNamespace(**kw)),
   642→        patch(_P_WRITE_QUERY, side_effect=_fake_write_query),
   643→    ):
   644→        do_prepare(
   645→            args,
   646→            state=state or {},
   647→            lang=SimpleNamespace(name="python"),
   648→            _state_path=None,
   649→            config=config or {},
   650→        )
   651→    return captured
   652→
   653→
   654→def test_review_prepare_zero_files_exits_with_error(capsys):
   655→    """Regression guard for issue #127: 0-file result must error, not silently succeed."""
   656→    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as exc:
   657→        _do_prepare_patched(total_files=0)
   658→    assert exc.value.code == 1
   659→    err = capsys.readouterr().err
   660→    assert "no files found" in err.lower()
   661→
   662→
   663→def test_review_prepare_zero_files_hints_scan_path(capsys):
   664→    """When state has a scan_path, the error hint mentions it."""
   665→    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
   666→        _do_prepare_patched(total_files=0, state={"scan_path": "."})
   667→    err = capsys.readouterr().err
   668→    assert "--path" in err
   669→
   670→
   671→def test_review_prepare_query_redacts_target_score():
   672→    captured = _do_prepare_patched(
   673→        total_files=3,
   674→        config={"target_strict_score": 98, "noise_budget": 10},
   675→    )
   676→
   677→    assert "config" in captured
   678→    config = captured["config"]
   679→    assert isinstance(config, dict)
   680→    assert "target_strict_score" not in config
   681→    assert config.get("noise_budget") == 10
   682→

   684→def test_require_batches_guides_rebuild_when_packet_has_no_batches(capsys):
   685→    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as exc:
   686→        _require_batches(
   687→            {"investigation_batches": []},

   690→        )
   691→    assert exc.value.code == 1
   692→    err = capsys.readouterr().err
   693→    assert "no investigation_batches" in err
   694→    assert "Regenerate review context first" in err
   695→    assert "review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import" in err
```

```
    75→def _merge_batch_results(batch_results: list[object]) -> dict[str, object]:
    76→    """Deterministically merge assessments/findings across batch outputs."""
    77→    normalized_results: list[dict] = []
    78→    for result in batch_results:

    80→            payload = result.to_dict()
    81→            if isinstance(payload, dict):

   286→
   287→def _validate_run_dir(run_dir: Path) -> tuple[dict, Path, str]:
   288→    """Validate run directory, load summary, and return (summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path).
   289→
   290→    Exits with error message on any validation failure.
   291→    """
   292→    if not run_dir.is_dir():
   293→        print(colorize(f"  Error: run directory not found: {run_dir}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   294→        sys.exit(1)
   295→
   296→    summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   297→    if not summary_path.exists():
   298→        print(colorize(f"  Error: no run_summary.json in {run_dir}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   299→        sys.exit(1)
   300→    try:
   301→        summary = json.loads(summary_path.read_text())
   302→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   303→        print(colorize(f"  Error reading run summary: {exc}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   304→        sys.exit(1)
   305→
   306→    successful = summary.get("successful_batches", [])
   307→    blind_packet_path = Path(str(summary.get("blind_packet", "")))
   308→    immutable_packet_path = str(summary.get("immutable_packet", ""))
   309→
   310→    if not successful:
   311→        print(colorize("  Error: no successful batches in run summary.", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   312→        sys.exit(1)
   313→    if not blind_packet_path.exists():
   314→        print(colorize(f"  Error: blind packet not found: {blind_packet_path}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   315→        sys.exit(1)
   316→
   317→    try:
   318→        packet = json.loads(Path(immutable_packet_path).read_text())
   319→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   320→        print(colorize(f"  Error reading immutable packet: {exc}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   321→        sys.exit(1)
   322→
   323→    summary["_packet"] = packet
   324→    return summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path
   325→
   326→
   327→def do_import_run(
   328→    run_dir_path: str,
   329→    state: dict,
   330→    lang,
   331→    state_file: str,
   332→    *,
   333→    config: dict | None = None,
   334→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   335→    scan_after_import: bool = False,
   336→    scan_path: str = ".",
   337→) -> None:
   338→    """Re-import results from a completed run directory.
   339→
   340→    Replays the merge+provenance+import step that normally runs at the end of
   341→    ``--run-batches``.  Useful when the original pipeline was interrupted (e.g.
   342→    broken pipe from background execution) but all batch results completed.
   343→    """
   344→    run_dir = Path(run_dir_path)
   345→    summary, blind_packet_path, _immutable_path = _validate_run_dir(run_dir)
   346→
   347→    runner = str(summary.get("runner", "codex"))
   348→    stamp = str(summary.get("run_stamp", ""))
   349→    successful = summary.get("successful_batches", [])
   350→    packet = summary.pop("_packet", {})
   351→    allowed_dims = {str(d) for d in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(d, str)}
   352→
   353→    # -- locate and parse raw batch results --
   354→    results_dir = run_dir / "results"
   355→    selected_indexes = [idx - 1 for idx in successful]  # convert 1-based to 0-based
   356→    output_files = {
   357→        idx: results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"
   358→        for idx in selected_indexes
   359→    }
   360→
   361→    missing = [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes if not output_files[idx].exists()]
   362→    if missing:
   363→        print(colorize(f"  Error: missing result files for batches: {missing}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   364→        sys.exit(1)
   365→
   366→    batch_results, failures = runner_helpers_mod.collect_batch_results(
   367→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   368→        failures=[],
   369→        output_files=output_files,
   370→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   371→        extract_payload_fn=lambda raw: batch_core_mod.extract_json_payload(raw, log_fn=log),
   372→        normalize_result_fn=lambda payload, dims: batch_core_mod.normalize_batch_result(
   373→            payload,
   374→            dims,
   375→            max_batch_findings=max_batch_findings_for_dimension_count(len(dims)),
   376→            abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   377→        ),
   378→    )
   379→
   380→    if not batch_results:
   381→        print(colorize("  Error: no valid batch results could be parsed.", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   382→        sys.exit(1)
   383→
   384→    print(colorize(f"  Parsed {len(batch_results)} batch results from {run_dir}", "bold"))
   385→    if failures:
   386→        print(colorize(f"  Warning: {len(failures)} batches failed to parse: {[f + 1 for f in failures]}", "yellow"))
   387→
   388→    # -- merge --
   389→    merged = _merge_batch_results(batch_results)
   390→
   391→    # -- build provenance --
   392→    successful_indexes = [idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in set(failures)]
   393→    merged["provenance"] = runner_helpers_mod.build_batch_import_provenance(
   394→        runner=runner,
   395→        blind_packet_path=blind_packet_path,
   396→        run_stamp=stamp,
   397→        batch_indexes=successful_indexes,
   398→    )
   399→
   400→    # -- write merged output --
   401→    merged_path = run_dir / "holistic_findings_merged.json"
   402→    safe_write_text(merged_path, json.dumps(merged, indent=2) + "\n")
   403→    print(colorize(f"  Merged output: {merged_path}", "bold"))
   404→
   405→    # -- import with trusted source --
   406→    _do_import(
   407→        str(merged_path),
   408→        state,
   409→        lang,
   410→        state_file,
   411→        config=config,
   412→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   413→        trusted_assessment_source=True,
   414→        trusted_assessment_label=f"trusted import-run replay from {run_dir.name}",
   415→    )
   416→
   417→    # -- optional follow-up scan --
   418→    if scan_after_import:
   419→        lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) or str(getattr(lang, "lang", ""))
   420→        if lang_name:
   421→            runner_helpers_mod.run_followup_scan(
   422→                lang_name=lang_name,
   423→                scan_path=scan_path,
   424→                deps=runner_helpers_mod.FollowupScanDeps(
   425→                    project_root=_runtime_project_root(),
   426→                    timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   427→                    python_executable=sys.executable,
   428→                    subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   429→                    timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   430→                    colorize_fn=colorize,
   431→                ),
   432→            )
   433→
```

```
    79→def _print_review_quality(quality: object, *, colorize_fn) -> None:
    80→    """Render merged review quality summary when present."""
    81→    if not isinstance(quality, dict):
    82→        return
    83→    coverage = quality.get("dimension_coverage")
    84→    density = quality.get("evidence_density")

    86→        REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY
    87→    )
    88→    if not isinstance(high_missing_issue_note, int | float):
    89→        high_missing_issue_note = quality.get(
    90→            LEGACY_REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY
    91→        )
    92→    finding_pressure = quality.get("finding_pressure")
    93→    dims_with_findings = quality.get("dimensions_with_findings")
    94→    if not isinstance(coverage, int | float) or not isinstance(density, int | float):
    95→        return
    96→
    97→    pressure_segment = ""
    98→    if isinstance(finding_pressure, int | float) and isinstance(dims_with_findings, int):
    99→        pressure_segment = (
   100→            f", finding-pressure {float(finding_pressure):.2f} "
   101→            f"across {dims_with_findings} dims"
   102→        )
   103→    print(
   104→        colorize_fn(
   105→            "  Review quality: "
   106→            f"dimension coverage {float(coverage):.2f}, "
   107→            f"evidence density {float(density):.2f}, "
   108→            f"high-score-missing-issue-note {int(high_missing_issue_note or 0)}"
   109→            f"{pressure_segment}",
   110→            "dim",
   111→        )
   112→    )

   115→def _collect_reviewed_files_from_batches_impl(
   116→    *,
   117→    batches: list[dict[str, object]],
   118→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   119→) -> list[str]:
   120→    """Collect normalized file paths reviewed in the selected batch set."""
   121→    reviewed: list[str] = []
   122→    seen: set[str] = set()
   123→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   124→        if idx < 0 or idx >= len(batches):
   125→            continue
   126→        batch = batches[idx]
   127→        files = batch.get("files_to_read", [])
   128→        if not isinstance(files, list):
   129→            continue
   130→        for raw in files:
   131→            if not isinstance(raw, str):
   132→                continue
   133→            path = raw.strip().strip(",'\"")
   134→            if not path or path in {".", ".."}:
   135→                continue
   136→            if path.endswith("/"):
   137→                continue
   138→            if path in seen:
   139→                continue
   140→            seen.add(path)
   141→            reviewed.append(path)
   142→    return reviewed
   143→
   144→
   145→def _collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   146→    *,
   147→    batches: list[dict[str, object]],
   148→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   149→) -> list[str]:
   150→    """Compatibility wrapper for reviewed-file collection."""
   151→    return _collect_reviewed_files_from_batches_impl(
   152→        batches=batches,
   153→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   154→    )
   155→
   156→
   157→from .runtime.policy import resolve_batch_run_policy
   158→
   159→
   160→def _normalize_dimension_list_impl(raw: object) -> list[str]:
   161→    """Normalize dimension collections to a stable, de-duplicated list."""
   162→    if not isinstance(raw, list):
   163→        return []
   164→    out: list[str] = []
   165→    seen: set[str] = set()
   166→    for item in raw:
   167→        if not isinstance(item, str):
   168→            continue
   169→        dim = item.strip()
   170→        if not dim or dim in seen:
   171→            continue
   172→        seen.add(dim)
   173→        out.append(dim)
   174→    return out
   175→
   176→
   177→def _normalize_dimension_list(raw: object) -> list[str]:
   178→    """Compatibility wrapper for dimension list normalization."""
   179→    return _normalize_dimension_list_impl(raw)
   180→
   181→
   182→def _scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name: str) -> list[str]:
   183→    """Return default scored subjective dimensions for one language."""
   184→    try:
   185→        default_dims, _, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   186→    except (ValueError, RuntimeError):
   187→        return []
   188→    return _normalize_dimension_list(default_dims)
   189→
   190→
   191→def _missing_scored_dimensions(
   192→    *,
   193→    selected_dims: list[str],
   194→    scored_dims: list[str],
   195→) -> list[str]:
   196→    selected = set(selected_dims)
   197→    return [dim for dim in scored_dims if dim not in selected]
   198→
   199→
   200→def _missing_dimensions_command(*, missing_dims: list[str], scan_path: str) -> str:
   201→    """Return rerun command for missing subjective dimensions."""
   202→    base = "desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import"
   203→    if scan_path and scan_path != ".":
   204→        base += f" --path {shlex.quote(scan_path)}"
   205→    if missing_dims:
   206→        base += f" --dimensions {','.join(missing_dims)}"
   207→    return base
   208→
   209→
   210→def _print_preflight_dimension_scope_notice_impl(
   211→    *,
   212→    selected_dims: list[str],
   213→    scored_dims: list[str],
   214→    explicit_selection: bool,
   215→    scan_path: str,
   216→    colorize_fn,
   217→) -> None:
   218→    """Print trigger-time notice when run scope is a scored-dimension subset."""
   219→    if not scored_dims:
   220→        return
   221→    missing_dims = _missing_scored_dimensions(
   222→        selected_dims=selected_dims,
   223→        scored_dims=scored_dims,
   224→    )
   225→    if not missing_dims:
   226→        return
   227→
   228→    covered_count = len([dim for dim in selected_dims if dim in set(scored_dims)])
   229→    scope_reason = (
   230→        "explicit --dimensions selection"
   231→        if explicit_selection
   232→        else "language default review dimension set"
   233→    )
   234→    tone = "yellow" if explicit_selection else "red"
   235→    print(
   236→        colorize_fn(
   237→            "  WARNING: this run targets "
   238→            f"{covered_count}/{len(scored_dims)} scored subjective dimensions "
   239→            f"({scope_reason}).",
   240→            tone,
   241→        )
   242→    )
   243→    preview = ", ".join(missing_dims[:5])
   244→    if len(missing_dims) > 5:
   245→        preview = f"{preview}, +{len(missing_dims) - 5} more"
   246→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Missing from this run: {preview}", "yellow"))
   247→    print(
   248→        colorize_fn(
   249→            "  Rerun missing dimensions: "
   250→            f"`{_missing_dimensions_command(missing_dims=missing_dims, scan_path=scan_path)}`",
   251→            "dim",
   252→        )
```

```
    21→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    22→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    23→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"

    29→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    30→# Grouping key computation
    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→
    33→def _extract_subtype(finding: dict) -> str | None:
    34→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a finding.
    35→
    36→    Checks detail.kind first, then falls back to parsing the finding ID
    37→    (format: ``detector::file::subtype`` or ``detector::file::symbol::subtype``).
    38→    """
    39→    detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    40→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    41→    if kind:
    42→        return kind
    43→
    44→    # Parse from finding ID — third segment is often the subtype
    45→    fid = finding.get("id", "")
    46→    parts = fid.split("::")
    47→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    48→        # For IDs like smells::file.py::silent_except, the subtype is parts[2]
    49→        # For IDs like dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read, subtype is last part
    50→        candidate = parts[-1]
    51→        # Skip if it looks like a file path or symbol name (contains / or .)
    52→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    53→            return candidate
    54→    return None
    55→
    56→
    57→def _grouping_key(finding: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    58→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a finding.
    59→
    60→    Returns None if the finding should not be auto-clustered.
    61→    """
    62→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    63→
    64→    if meta is None:
    65→        # Unknown detector — group by detector name
    66→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    67→
    68→    # Review findings → group by dimension
    69→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    70→        detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    71→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    72→        if dimension:
    73→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    74→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    75→
    76→    # Per-file detectors (structural, responsibility_cohesion)
    77→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in (
    78→        "structural", "responsibility_cohesion",
    79→    ):
    80→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    81→        if file:
    82→            basename = os.path.basename(file)
    83→            return f"file::{detector}::{basename}"
    84→
    85→    # Needs-judgment detectors — group by subtype when available
    86→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    87→        subtype = _extract_subtype(finding)
    88→        if subtype:
    89→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    90→
    91→    # Pure auto-fix (no judgment needed) → all findings by detector
    92→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    93→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    94→
    95→    # Everything else → by detector
    96→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    97→
    98→
    99→def _cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
   100→    """Convert a grouping key to a cluster name with auto/ prefix."""
   101→    # auto::unused → auto/unused
   102→    # typed::dict_keys::phantom_read → auto/dict_keys-phantom_read
   103→    # file::structural::big_file.py → auto/structural-big_file.py
   104→    # review::abstraction_fitness → auto/review-abstraction_fitness
   105→    # detector::security → auto/security
   106→    parts = key.split("::")
   107→    if len(parts) == 2:
   108→        prefix_type = parts[0]
   109→        # For review keys, keep the prefix for clarity
   110→        if prefix_type == "review":
   111→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}review-{parts[1]}"
   112→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}"
   113→    if len(parts) == 3:
   114→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{parts[2]}"
   115→    return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{key.replace('::', '-')}"
   116→
   117→
   118→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   119→# Description and action generation
   120→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121→
   122→def _generate_description(
   123→    cluster_name: str,
   124→    members: list[dict],
   125→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   126→    subtype: str | None,
   127→) -> str:
   128→    """Generate a human-readable cluster description."""
   129→    count = len(members)
   130→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   131→
   132→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
   133→        detail = (members[0].get("detail") or {}) if members else {}
   134→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", detector)
   135→        return f"Address {count} {dimension} review findings"
   136→
   137→    if detector == "structural":
   138→        files = {os.path.basename(m.get("file", "")) for m in members}
   139→        if len(files) == 1:
   140→            return f"Decompose {next(iter(files))}"
   141→        return f"Decompose {count} large files"
   142→
   143→    display = meta.display if meta else detector
   144→    if subtype:
   145→        label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
   146→        return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
   147→
   148→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
   149→        return f"Remove {count} {display} findings"
   150→
   151→    return f"Fix {count} {display} issues"
   152→
   153→
   154→def _subtype_has_fixer(meta: DetectorMeta, subtype: str | None) -> str | None:
   155→    """Check if a subtype maps to a specific fixer."""
   156→    if not meta.fixers or not subtype:
   157→        return None
   158→    # Convention: fixer name is subtype with _ replaced by -
   159→    fixer_name = subtype.replace("_", "-")
   160→    if fixer_name in meta.fixers:
   161→        return fixer_name
   162→    # Also check if subtype is a substring (e.g. "unused" matches "unused-imports")
   163→    for fixer in meta.fixers:
   164→        if subtype in fixer:
   165→            return fixer
   166→    return None
   167→
   168→
   169→def _strip_guidance_examples(guidance: str) -> str:
   170→    """Strip subtype-specific examples from guidance text.
   171→
   172→    Many guidance strings have format "verb — specific examples".
   173→    Strip after the dash to get the core action:
   174→      "fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains" → "fix code smells"
   175→    """
   176→    if " — " in guidance:
   177→        return guidance.split(" — ", 1)[0].strip()
   178→    return guidance
   179→
   180→
   181→_ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES: dict[str, str] = {
   182→    "reorganize": "reorganize with desloppify move",
   183→    "refactor": "review and refactor each finding",
   184→    "manual_fix": "review and fix each finding",
   185→}
   186→
   187→
   188→def _generate_action(
   189→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   190→    subtype: str | None,
   191→) -> str:
   192→    """Generate an action string from detector metadata.
   193→
   194→    Always returns a non-empty string — every cluster gets an action.
   195→    """
   196→    if meta is None:
   197→        return "review and fix each finding"
   198→
   199→    # For detectors with subtypes, only suggest a fixer if the subtype matches
   200→    if subtype and meta.fixers:
   201→        matched_fixer = _subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
   202→        if matched_fixer:
   203→            return f"desloppify fix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
   204→    elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
   205→        # Pure auto-fix detector, no subtypes
   206→        return f"desloppify fix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   207→
   208→    if meta.tool == "move":
   209→        return "desloppify move"
   210→
   211→    # Guidance available — use it (strip examples for subtyped detectors)
   212→    if meta.guidance:
   213→        if subtype:
   214→            return _strip_guidance_examples(meta.guidance)
   215→        return meta.guidance
   216→
   217→    # Final fallback: action_type template
   218→    return _ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES.get(
   219→        meta.action_type, "review and fix each finding"
   220→    )
   221→
   222→
   223→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   224→# Repair
   225→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   226→
   227→def _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan: PlanModel, now: str) -> int:
   228→    """Clear override cluster refs that point to non-existent clusters."""
   229→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   230→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   231→    repaired = 0
   232→    for override in overrides.values():
   233→        cluster_name = override.get("cluster")
   234→        if cluster_name and cluster_name not in clusters:
   235→            override["cluster"] = None
   236→            override["updated_at"] = now
   237→            repaired += 1
   238→    return repaired
   239→

   241→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   242→# Main algorithm
   243→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   244→
   245→def auto_cluster_findings(
   246→    plan: PlanModel,
   247→    state: StateModel,
   248→    *,
   249→    target_strict: float = 95.0,
   250→) -> int:
   251→    """Regenerate auto-clusters from current open findings.
   252→
   253→    Returns count of changes made (clusters created, updated, or deleted).
   254→    """
   255→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   256→    changes = 0
   257→
   258→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   259→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   260→
   261→    # Set of finding IDs in manual (non-auto) clusters
   262→    manual_member_ids: set[str] = set()
   263→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   264→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   265→            manual_member_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   266→
   267→    # Collect open, non-suppressed findings and group by key
   268→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   269→    finding_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
   270→    for fid, finding in findings.items():
   271→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
   272→            continue
   273→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   274→            continue
   275→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
   276→            continue
   277→
   278→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   279→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   280→        key = _grouping_key(finding, meta)
   281→        if key is None:
   282→            continue
   283→
   284→        groups[key].append(fid)
   285→        finding_data[fid] = finding
   286→
   287→    # Drop singleton groups
   288→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
   289→
   290→    # Map existing auto-clusters by cluster_key
   291→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str] = {}  # cluster_key → cluster_name
   292→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   293→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   294→            ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   295→            if ck:
   296→                existing_by_key[ck] = name
   297→
   298→    now = utc_now()
   299→    active_auto_keys: set[str] = set()
   300→
   301→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   302→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   303→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   304→
   305→        # Representative finding for metadata
   306→        rep = finding_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   307→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   308→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   309→        members = [finding_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in finding_data]
   310→
   311→        # Extract subtype from grouping key (typed::detector::subtype)
   312→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   313→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   314→
   315→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   316→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   317→
   318→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   319→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   320→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   321→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   322→                # Merge new findings in, don't remove user's edits
   323→                existing_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   324→                new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
   325→                if new_ids:
   326→                    cluster["finding_ids"].extend(new_ids)
   327→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   328→                    changes += 1
   329→            else:
   330→                # Replace membership wholesale
   331→                old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   332→                new_ids = set(member_ids)
   333→                if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   334→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   335→                    cluster["description"] = description
   336→                    cluster["action"] = action
   337→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   338→                    changes += 1
   339→        else:
   340→            # Handle name collision (existing auto-cluster with different key)
   341→            # If cluster_name already exists with a different key, skip
   342→            if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   343→                # Append a disambiguator
   344→                cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   345→
   346→            new_cluster: Cluster = {
   347→                "name": cluster_name,
   348→                "description": description,
   349→                "finding_ids": list(member_ids),
   350→                "created_at": now,
   351→                "updated_at": now,
   352→                "auto": True,
   353→                "cluster_key": key,
   354→                "action": action,
   355→                "user_modified": False,
   356→            }
   357→            clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   358→            existing_by_key[key] = cluster_name
   359→            changes += 1
   360→
   361→        # Update overrides to track cluster membership
   362→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   363→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(key, cluster_name)
   364→        for fid in member_ids:
   365→            if fid not in overrides:
   366→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   367→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   368→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   369→
   370→    # --- Subjective dimension clusters (unscored + stale) -------------------
   371→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   372→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   373→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   374→    )
   375→    unscored_state_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   376→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(

   380→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid not in unscored_state_ids

   382→
   383→    # -- Initial review cluster (unscored, min size 1) ---------------------
   384→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
```

## Analysis: Repetitive Boilerplate in `auto_cluster.py`

### 1. The Four Cluster Types

The file handles exactly 4 cluster types, each processing a different set of findings:

#### **A. Detector-based clusters** (lines 301–369)
- **Source**: Regular open findings grouped by `_grouping_key()`
- **Key pattern**: `detector::{name}`, `typed::{detector}::{subtype}`, `review::{dimension}`, etc.
- **Cluster name**: Generated via `_cluster_name_from_key()`
- **Min size**: 2 findings (`_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE`)
- **Description/Action**: Generated from detector metadata and subtype via `_generate_description()` and `_generate_action()`

#### **B. Unscored (initial-review) cluster** (lines 383–422)
- **Source**: Subjective dimension findings from `current_unscored_ids(state)`
- **Key**: `_UNSCORED_KEY` = `"subjective::unscored"`
- **Cluster name**: `_UNSCORED_NAME` = `"auto/initial-review"`
- **Min size**: 1 finding (`_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE`)
- **Description/Action**: Hardcoded with dimension CLI keys
  - Description: `f"Initial review of {len(...)} unscored subjective dimensions"`
  - Action: `f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"`

#### **C. Stale (re-review) cluster** (lines 424–467)
- **Source**: Subjective dimensions that were previously scored but are now stale
- **Key**: `_STALE_KEY` = `"subjective::stale"`
- **Cluster name**: `_STALE_NAME` = `"auto/stale-review"`
- **Min size**: 2 findings
- **Description/Action**: Hardcoded with dimension CLI keys
  - Description: `f"Re-review {len(...)} stale subjective dimensions"`
  - Action: Similar but with `--force-review-rerun` flag

#### **D. Under-target cluster** (lines 469–547)
- **Source**: Subjective dimensions currently scored but below target threshold
- **Key**: `_UNDER_TARGET_KEY` = `"subjective::under-target"`
- **Cluster name**: `_UNDER_TARGET_NAME` = `"auto/under-target-review"`
- **Min size**: 2 findings
- **Min size**: 2 findings
- **Special feature**: `optional: True` field set on cluster
- **Description/Action**: Hardcoded with dimension CLI keys
  - Description: `f"Consider re-reviewing {len(...)} dimensions under target score"`
  - Action: Similar with `--force-review-rerun` flag
- **Extra logic**: Prunes previous members that improved above threshold, injects new IDs into `queue_order`

---

### 2. The Common Pattern Across All Four Types

Every cluster type follows this identical sequence:

```python
# Step 1: Compute/prepare data (grouped findings or dimension IDs)
# Step 2: Generate cluster_name, description, action
# Step 3: Check if cluster already exists
if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
    cluster = clusters[existing_name]
    # Step 4a: Sync membership (compare old_ids vs new_ids)
    old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    new_ids = set(member_ids)
    # Step 4b: Update metadata only if changed
    if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
        cluster["finding_ids"] = list(member_ids)
        cluster["description"] = description
        cluster["action"] = action
        cluster["updated_at"] = now
        changes += 1
else:
    # Step 5: Create new cluster
    new_cluster = {
        "name": cluster_name,
        "description": description,
        "finding_ids": list(member_ids),
        "created_at": now,
        "updated_at": now,
        "auto": True,
        "cluster_key": key,
        "action": action,
        "user_modified": False,
    }
    clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
    existing_by_key[key] = cluster_name
    changes += 1

# Step 6: Sync overrides (track cluster membership per finding)
overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
current_name = existing_by_key.get(key, cluster_name)
for fid in member_ids:
    if fid not in overrides:
        overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
    overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
    overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
```

**This 6-step pattern repeats identically for all 4 types**, with only these variations:
- Input data preparation (groups vs dimension IDs)
- Cluster name generation
- Description/action generation
- Optional flags (e.g., `optional: True` for under-target)
- Optional queue_order manipulation (under-target only)

---

### 3. Differences Between Each Cluster Type

| Aspect | Detector-based | Unscored | Stale | Under-target |
|--------|--|--|--|--|
| **Source data** | `groups[key]` (from findings) | `unscored_queue_ids` | `stale_queue_ids` | `under_target_queue_ids` |
| **Key type** | Dynamic (computed per group) | Fixed (`_UNSCORED_KEY`) | Fixed (`_STALE_KEY`) | Fixed (`_UNDER_TARGET_KEY`) |
| **Min size** | 2 | 1 | 2 | 2 |
| **Metadata generation** | Via `_generate_description()`, `_generate_action(meta, subtype)` | Hardcoded description/action | Hardcoded description/action | Hardcoded description/action |
| **Has optional flag** | No | No | No | Yes (`optional: True`) |
| **Queue manipulation** | None | None | None | Adds missing IDs to `queue_order` |
| **User-modify aware** | Yes (checks `user_modified`, merges instead of replacing) | N/A | N/A | N/A |
| **Code lines** | 301–369 (69 lines) | 383–422 (40 lines) | 424–467 (44 lines) | 469–547 (79 lines) |

---

### 4. Shared Helper Function Signature

Based on the pattern, here's what a unified helper would look like:

```python
def _sync_or_create_auto_cluster(
    clusters: dict[str, Cluster],
    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
    cluster_key: str,
    cluster_name: str,
    member_ids: list[str],
    description: str,
    action: str,
    plan: PlanModel,
    now: str,
    *,
    min_size: int = 2,
    optional: bool = False,
    user_modify_aware: bool = False,
    queue_injection: list[str] | None = None,
) -> int:
    """Sync or create an auto-cluster with common boilerplate handling.
    
    Args:
        clusters: The clusters dict from plan
        existing_by_key: Mapping of cluster_key to cluster_name
        cluster_key: The unique cluster key (e.g., "auto::unused", "subjective::unscored")
        cluster_name: The display name for the cluster
        member_ids: List of finding IDs for this cluster
        description: Human-readable description
        action: Action string (CLI command suggestion)
        plan: Full plan model (for overrides and queue_order)
        now: Current timestamp (utc_now())
        min_size: Minimum size to create/keep cluster (default 2)
        optional: Whether to set optional=True on new cluster
        user_modify_aware: Whether to check user_modified flag and merge instead of replace
        queue_injection: Finding IDs to add to queue_order (under-target only)
        
    Returns:
        Number of changes made (0, 1, or more)
    """
```

**Parameter breakdown:**
- **Inputs**: All data needed to sync/create (key, name, members, metadata)
- **Config flags**: `min_size`, `optional`, `user_modify_aware`, `queue_injection` to capture the 4 type variations
- **Output**: Change count for accumulation into the main `changes` variable

---

### 5. `auto_cluster_findings` Signature, Flow, and Return Type

**Signature (lines 245–250):**
```python
def auto_cluster_findings(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    target_strict: float = 95.0,
) -> int:
```

**Parameters:**
- `plan` (PlanModel): The living plan dict with clusters, overrides, queue_order
- `state` (StateModel): The state dict with findings and scoring data
- `target_strict` (float, keyword-only): Score threshold for under-target detection (default 95.0)

**Return type:** `int` — total count of changes made (cluster creates, updates, deletes, override syncs)

**Flow (30k-foot view):**

1. **Ensure plan defaults** (line 255)
2. **Collect auto-clusterable findings** (lines 258–288):
   - Exclude manual-cluster members, non-open, suppressed
   - Group by key
   - Drop singleton groups
3. **Sync detector-based clusters** (lines 290–369):
   - For each group, sync/create cluster, update overrides
4. **Extract subjective dimension IDs** (lines 370–381):
   - Partition all subjective IDs into unscored vs stale
5. **Sync unscored cluster** (lines 383–422):
   - If >= 1 unscored ID, sync/create
6. **Sync stale cluster** (lines 424–467):
   - If >= 2 stale IDs, sync/create
7. **Sync under-target cluster** (lines 469–547):
   - Prune previous members that improved
   - If >= 2 under-target IDs, sync/create and inject into queue_order
8. **Clean stale auto-clusters** (lines 549–578):
   - Delete clusters with no active group, respecting user_modified flag
9. **Repair ghost refs** (line 581):
   - Clear orphaned override cluster references
10. **Mark plan updated** (line 584)
11. **Return total changes** (line 585)

**Key insight**: The function is purely **regenerative** — it completely recomputes all auto-clusters from scratch each time, idempotently diffing against existing clusters to count only real changes.

---

## Summary Table

| Aspect | Value |
|--------|-------|
| **Cluster types** | 4 (detector-based, unscored, stale, under-target) |
| **Common pattern** | 6-step: prepare data → generate metadata → check exists → sync membership → update metadata → sync overrides |
| **Boilerplate repetition** | Lines 301–369, 383–422, 424–467, 469–547 (233 lines total, ~58 lines per type) |
| **Suggested helper params** | 11 params: clusters, existing_by_key, cluster_key, cluster_name, member_ids, description, action, plan, now, min_size, optional, user_modify_aware, queue_injection |
| **Main function signature** | `auto_cluster_findings(plan, state, *, target_strict=95.0) -> int` |
| **Return semantics** | Total change count (idempotent regeneration) |

```
1→"""Shared runtime path resolution for review command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.text_api import get_project_root
     8→
     9→
    10→def runtime_project_root(*, project_root_override: Path | None = None) -> Path:
    11→    """Resolve project root with optional test override hook."""
    12→    if isinstance(project_root_override, Path):
    13→        return project_root_override
    14→    return get_project_root()
    15→
    16→
    17→def review_packet_dir(
    18→    *,
    19→    project_root_override: Path | None = None,
    20→    review_packet_dir_override: Path | None = None,
    21→) -> Path:
    22→    """Resolve `.desloppify/review_packets` with optional override."""
    23→    if isinstance(review_packet_dir_override, Path):
    24→        return review_packet_dir_override
    25→    return runtime_project_root(project_root_override=project_root_override) / ".desloppify" / "review_packets"
    26→
    27→
    28→def blind_packet_path(
    29→    *,
    30→    project_root_override: Path | None = None,
    31→    stamp: str | None = None,
    32→) -> Path:
    33→    """Resolve blind packet path under `.desloppify`.
    34→
    35→    When ``stamp`` is provided, return a run-scoped blind packet path to avoid
    36→    cross-run write races.
    37→    """
    38→    if isinstance(stamp, str) and stamp.strip():
    39→        packet_dir = review_packet_dir(project_root_override=project_root_override)
    40→        return packet_dir / f"review_packet_blind_{stamp.strip()}.json"
    41→    return runtime_project_root(project_root_override=project_root_override) / ".desloppify" / "review_packet_blind.json"
    42→
    43→
    44→def subagent_runs_dir(
    45→    *,
    46→    project_root_override: Path | None = None,
    47→    subagent_runs_dir_override: Path | None = None,
    48→) -> Path:
    49→    """Resolve subagent run artifact directory with optional override."""
    50→    if isinstance(subagent_runs_dir_override, Path):
    51→        return subagent_runs_dir_override
    52→    return runtime_project_root(project_root_override=project_root_override) / ".desloppify" / "subagents" / "runs"
    53→
    54→
    55→def external_session_root(
    56→    *,
    57→    project_root_override: Path | None = None,
    58→    external_session_root_override: Path | None = None,
    59→) -> Path:
    60→    """Resolve external review session root with optional override."""
    61→    if isinstance(external_session_root_override, Path):
    62→        return external_session_root_override
    63→    return runtime_project_root(project_root_override=project_root_override) / ".desloppify" / "external_review_sessions"
    64→
    65→
    66→__all__ = [
    67→    "blind_packet_path",
    68→    "external_session_root",
    69→    "review_packet_dir",
    70→    "runtime_project_root",
    71→    "subagent_runs_dir",
    72→]
```

Perfect! Now I have the complete picture. Let me create a comprehensive summary report.

## Analysis Complete

Based on my investigation of the codebase, here's what I found:

### 1. **Test Import Issue**
The test file imports `ReviewBatchRuntimePaths` from `desloppify.app.commands.review.batch`:
```python
from desloppify.app.commands.review.batch import (
    ReviewBatchRuntimePaths,
    _do_run_batches,
)
```

**Result**: This class **does not exist** anywhere in the codebase.

### 2. **What the Test Expects**
The test uses `ReviewBatchRuntimePaths` as a data container with three attributes:
- `project_root: Path`
- `review_packet_dir: Path`  
- `subagent_runs_dir: Path`

Example usage:
```python
_do_run_batches(
    args, empty_state, lang, "fake_sp", 
    config={}, 
    runtime_paths=ReviewBatchRuntimePaths(
        project_root=tmp_path,
        review_packet_dir=review_packet_dir,
        subagent_runs_dir=runs_dir,
    )
)
```

### 3. **What Actually Exists**
- **`batch.py`** has `_do_run_batches()` function with signature:
  ```python
  def _do_run_batches(args, state, lang, state_file, config: dict | None = None) -> None:
  ```
  It does **NOT** accept a `runtime_paths` parameter.

- **`runtime_paths.py`** exists with utility functions for path resolution:
  - `runtime_project_root()`
  - `review_packet_dir()`
  - `blind_packet_path()`
  - `subagent_runs_dir()`
  - `external_session_root()`
  
  But **no `ReviewBatchRuntimePaths` class** is defined here.

- **`batches.py`** has `do_run_batches()` which DOES accept explicit parameters:
  ```python
  def do_run_batches(
      args, state, lang, state_file, *,
      config: dict | None,
      ...many function parameters...,
      project_root: Path,
      subagent_runs_dir: Path,
  ) -> None:
  ```

### 4. **The Current Implementation**
In `batch.py`, `_do_run_batches()` calls `batches.do_run_batches()` and passes:
```python
return review_batches_mod.do_run_batches(
    args,
    state,
    lang,
    state_file,
    config=config,
    ...many function parameters...,
    project_root=runtime_project_root,      # Line 282
    subagent_runs_dir=_subagent_runs_dir(), # Line 283
)
```

### 5. **The Minimal Fix**
You need to define `ReviewBatchRuntimePaths` as a simple data class in `batch.py`. The fix is straightforward:

**Option A** (Lightweight - NamedTuple):
```python
from typing import NamedTuple

class ReviewBatchRuntimePaths(NamedTuple):
    project_root: Path
    review_packet_dir: Path
    subagent_runs_dir: Path
```

**Option B** (Modern - dataclass):
```python
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass
class ReviewBatchRuntimePaths:
    project_root: Path
    review_packet_dir: Path
    subagent_runs_dir: Path
```

Then modify `_do_run_batches()` to accept an optional `runtime_paths` parameter:
```python
def _do_run_batches(args, state, lang, state_file, config: dict | None = None, runtime_paths: ReviewBatchRuntimePaths | None = None) -> None:
```

And use it to override the helper functions:
```python
def _runtime_project_root() -> Path:
    if runtime_paths and isinstance(runtime_paths.project_root, Path):
        return runtime_paths.project_root
    if isinstance(PROJECT_ROOT, Path):
        return PROJECT_ROOT
    return get_project_root()

def _subagent_runs_dir() -> Path:
    if runtime_paths and isinstance(runtime_paths.subagent_runs_dir, Path):
        return runtime_paths.subagent_runs_dir
    if isinstance(SUBAGENT_RUNS_DIR, Path):
        return SUBAGENT_RUNS_DIR
    return _runtime_project_root() / ".desloppify" / "subagents" / "runs"
```

**Absolute File Paths Referenced:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/review/integration/review_commands_cases.py` (test file with failing imports)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batch.py` (where fix is needed)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/runtime_paths.py` (alternative location, not the solution)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batches.py` (where `do_run_batches` is defined)
